

================================================================================
FILE_PATH: ./run_mvp.py

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed
import dataclasses
import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import yaml

from src.brain.memory_tms import EpisodicMemoryTMS
from src.core.contracts import (
    FactorialCellSpec,
    PolicyMode,
    RepEMode,
    SimulationConfig,
)
from src.environment.game_master import GameMaster, TickMacroSummary
from src.evaluation.metrics import (
    EconomyMetricsCollector,
    EconomySeedClusterMetrics,
    ExogenousWindfallMPCProbe,
)
from src.evaluation.validity_and_stats import (
    clustered_paired_contrast,
    compute_2x2x2_factorial_effects_and_shapley,
)
from src.orchestration.turn_orchestrator import TurnOrchestrator


# ============================================================================
# 1. COMPLETE 2^3 FACTORIAL ABLATION MATRIX + MATCHED SCIENTIFIC CONTROLS
# ============================================================================

def build_full_factorial_and_control_specs() -> Dict[str, FactorialCellSpec]:
    """
    Constructs all 8 cells of the 2^3 Factorial Ablation Matrix (C000..C111)
    plus the 4 mandatory mechanistic and non-LLM baselines:
    - Factor A: Bounded Deliberation Budget (B_CoT)
    - Factor B: Episodic Memory Friction (M_RAG)
    - Factor C: Two-Stage Endogenous Probe-Gated RepE (R_RepE)
    """
    specs: Dict[str, FactorialCellSpec] = {}
    for a in (0, 1):
        for b in (0, 1):
            for c in (0, 1):
                cid = f"C{a}{b}{c}"
                specs[cid] = FactorialCellSpec(
                    cell_id=cid,
                    deliberation_bottleneck=bool(a),
                    memory_friction=bool(b),
                    endogenous_repe=bool(c),
                    repe_mode=(
                        RepEMode.ENDOGENOUS_PROBE_GATED if c == 1 else RepEMode.OFF
                    ),
                    policy_mode=PolicyMode.LLM_AGENT,
                )

    # Mandatory Mechanistic Controls (Paired on identical seeds)
    specs["CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC"] = FactorialCellSpec(
        cell_id="CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC",
        deliberation_bottleneck=True,
        memory_friction=True,
        endogenous_repe=True,
        repe_mode=RepEMode.PLACEBO_RANDOM_VECTOR,
        policy_mode=PolicyMode.LLM_AGENT,
    )
    specs["CTRL_SHUFFLED_LAMBDA"] = FactorialCellSpec(
        cell_id="CTRL_SHUFFLED_LAMBDA",
        deliberation_bottleneck=True,
        memory_friction=True,
        endogenous_repe=True,
        repe_mode=RepEMode.SHUFFLED_LAMBDA,
        policy_mode=PolicyMode.LLM_AGENT,
    )

    # Non-LLM Baseline Comparators (Empirical Null Floor & Closed-Form Reference)
    specs["BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE"] = FactorialCellSpec(
        cell_id="BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE",
        deliberation_bottleneck=False,
        memory_friction=False,
        endogenous_repe=False,
        repe_mode=RepEMode.OFF,
        policy_mode=PolicyMode.ZERO_INTELLIGENCE,
    )
    specs["BASE_CLOSED_FORM"] = FactorialCellSpec(
        cell_id="BASE_CLOSED_FORM",
        deliberation_bottleneck=False,
        memory_friction=False,
        endogenous_repe=False,
        repe_mode=RepEMode.OFF,
        policy_mode=PolicyMode.CLOSED_FORM_BENCHMARK,
    )
    return specs


# ============================================================================
# 2. SINGLE ECONOMY SEED EXECUTOR (ISOLATED CLUSTER WORKER)
# ============================================================================

def execute_single_economy_seed(
    cell_cfg: SimulationConfig,
    economy_seed: int,
    llm_controller: Any = None,
    eval_windfall_mpc: bool = True,
    output_dir: Optional[str] = None,
) -> EconomySeedClusterMetrics:
    """
    Runs one completely self-contained economy cluster (burn_in + active ticks)
    for `(cell_cfg.factorial_cell.cell_id, economy_seed)`.
    """
    cell_id = cell_cfg.factorial_cell.cell_id
    run_id = f"{cell_cfg.experiment_id}__{cell_id}__seed{economy_seed:03d}"

    gm = GameMaster(config=cell_cfg, economy_seed=economy_seed)
    mem_tms = EpisodicMemoryTMS(
        config=cell_cfg.memory_friction,
        db_path=":memory:",
        use_neural_embeddings=False,
    )
    orch = TurnOrchestrator(
        config=cell_cfg,
        game_master=gm,
        memory_tms=mem_tms,
        llm_controller=llm_controller,
        run_id=run_id,
        economy_seed=economy_seed,
    )

    total_ticks = cell_cfg.burn_in_ticks + cell_cfg.active_ticks
    post_burn_in_summaries: List[TickMacroSummary] = []

    windfall_mpcs: Optional[List[float]] = None
    windfall_counts: Optional[List[int]] = None
    windfall_trigger_tick = cell_cfg.burn_in_ticks + max(
        1, min(5, cell_cfg.active_ticks // 2)
    )

    for tick in range(1, total_ticks + 1):
        # Execute paired exogenous windfall MPC probe cleanly without mutating main economy
        if eval_windfall_mpc and tick == windfall_trigger_tick:
            windfall_mpcs, windfall_counts = (
                ExogenousWindfallMPCProbe.run_paired_windfall_probe(
                    orchestrator=orch,
                    start_tick=tick,
                    windfall_amount=20.0,
                    horizon_ticks=3,
                )
            )

        summary = orch.run_tick(tick)
        if tick > cell_cfg.burn_in_ticks:
            post_burn_in_summaries.append(summary)

    # Persist turn-level audit log if requested
    if output_dir is not None:
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        turn_log_path = os.path.join(
            output_dir, f"turns_{cell_id}_s{economy_seed:03d}.jsonl"
        )
        with open(turn_log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for rec in orch.turn_records:
                f.write(rec.model_dump_json() + "\n")

    return EconomyMetricsCollector.summarize_single_economy_seed(
        config=cell_cfg,
        economy_seed=economy_seed,
        orchestrator=orch,
        post_burn_in_summaries=post_burn_in_summaries,
        windfall_mpc_terciles=windfall_mpcs,
        windfall_mpc_counts=windfall_counts,
    )


def _parallel_non_llm_worker(
    args_tuple: Tuple[Dict[str, Any], int, bool, Optional[str]]
) -> EconomySeedClusterMetrics:
    raw_cfg, seed, eval_mpc, out_dir = args_tuple
    cfg = SimulationConfig(**raw_cfg)
    return execute_single_economy_seed(
        cell_cfg=cfg,
        economy_seed=seed,
        llm_controller=None,
        eval_windfall_mpc=eval_mpc,
        output_dir=out_dir,
    )


# ============================================================================
# 3. CLI ENTRYPOINT & MASTER ABLATION ORCHESTRATOR
# ============================================================================

def _resolve_git_sha() -> str:
    try:
        return (
            subprocess.check_output(
                ["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"],
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                text=True,
            ).strip()
            or "unknown"
        )
    except Exception:
        return "unknown"


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Neuropolis v2: 2^3 Factorial Ablation & Multi-Seed Clustered Evaluation Runner"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--config",
        type=str,
        default="configs/mvp_config.yaml",
        help="Path to SimulationConfig YAML file.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--cells",
        type=str,
        default="BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE,BASE_CLOSED_FORM",
        help=(
            "Comma-separated cell IDs to execute, or 'ALL' (all 8 factorial cells + 4 controls), "
            "or 'FACTORIAL_8' (C000..C111), or 'BASELINES' (ZI + Closed-Form)."
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--seeds",
        type=int,
        default=30,
        help="Number of independent economy seeds M (M >= 30 required for confirmatory power).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--allow-dev-seeds",
        action="store_true",
        help="Permit M < 30 for rapid local debugging (stamps output as DEV_UNDERPOWERED).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--num-agents",
        type=int,
        default=None,
        help="Override population size N per economy.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--active-ticks",
        type=int,
        default=None,
        help="Override post-burn-in active ticks per economy.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--burn-in-ticks",
        type=int,
        default=None,
        help="Override burn-in ticks per economy.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--workers",
        type=int,
        default=4,
        help="Parallel CPU workers for non-LLM baseline cells (ZI / Closed-Form).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--skip-windfall-mpc",
        action="store_true",
        help="Skip the paired exogenous windfall MPC shadow probe.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--save-turn-logs",
        action="store_true",
        help="Write per-turn JSONL audit logs for every seed (disabled by default for speed).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir",
        type=str,
        default="results/neuropolis_v2",
        help="Untracked output directory for manifests and statistical reports.",
    )
    return parser.parse_args()


def _print_report_summary(
    cell_reports: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
    factorial_analysis: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
    paired_contrasts: Dict[str, Any],
    m_seeds: int,
) -> None:
    power_banner = (
        f"CONFIRMATORY POWER ADEQUATE (M={m_seeds} >= 30)"
        if m_seeds >= 30
        else f"DEV_UNDERPOWERED (M={m_seeds} < 30 — EXPLORATORY ONLY)"
    )
    print("\n" + "=" * 114)
    print(f" NEUROPOLIS v2 — CLUSTERED EVALUATION SUMMARY [{power_banner}]")
    print("=" * 114)
    print(
        f"{'Cell ID':<24} | {'M':>3} | {'Valid%':>6} | {'Surv%':>6} | {'Freeze%':>7} | "
        f"{'Barg W1 (95% CI)':>23} | {'HtM JSD':>7} | {'W-HtM%':>6} | {'Max SFC Err':>11}"
    )
    print("-" * 114)
    for cid, rep in cell_reports.items():
        w1_info = rep["bargaining_w1"]
        w1_str = f"{w1_info['debiased_w1']:.4f} [{w1_info['ci_95'][0]:.3f},{w1_info['ci_95'][1]:.3f}]"
        jsd_val = rep["htm_miller_madow_jsd"]["debiased_jsd"]
        whtm_bin = next(
            (b for b in rep["htm_per_bin_tost"] if b["bin_label"] == "Wealthy_HtM"),
            None,
        )
        whtm_mean = whtm_bin["sim_cluster_mean"] * 100.0 if whtm_bin else 0.0
        print(
            f"{cid:<24} | {rep['n_economy_seeds_m']:>3d} | "
            f"{rep['json_valid_rate']*100:>5.1f}% | {rep['survival_rate']*100:>5.1f}% | "
            f"{rep['market_freeze_rate']*100:>6.1f}% | {w1_str:>23} | "
            f"{jsd_val:>7.4f} | {whtm_mean:>5.1f}% | {rep['max_sfc_conservation_error']:>11.2e}"
        )

    print("\n" + "-" * 114)
    print(" PER-BIN TOST (INTERSECTION-UNION TEST, HOLM-BONFERRONI ADJUSTED) — TWO-ASSET HtM & BARGAINING")
    print("-" * 114)
    for cid, rep in cell_reports.items():
        equiv_barg = sum(
            1 for b in rep["bargaining_per_bin_tost"] if b["statistically_equivalent"]
        )
        equiv_htm = sum(
            1 for b in rep["htm_per_bin_tost"] if b["statistically_equivalent"]
        )
        equiv_mpc = sum(
            1 for b in rep["windfall_mpc_per_bin_tost"] if b["statistically_equivalent"]
        )
        print(
            f"  {cid:<24}: Bargaining TOST Equiv Bins = {equiv_barg}/4 | "
            f"HtM TOST Equiv Bins = {equiv_htm}/3 | Windfall MPC TOST Equiv Bins = {equiv_mpc}/3"
        )

    if paired_contrasts:
        print("\n" + "-" * 114)
        print(" COMMON RANDOM NUMBERS (CRN) PAIRED CLUSTER CONTRASTS (Bargaining W1 Difference; Negative = Improvement)")
        print("-" * 114)
        for label, c_res in paired_contrasts.items():
            print(
                f"  {label:<44}: diff={c_res['paired_mean_diff']:+.4f} "
                f"(SE={c_res['paired_clustered_se']:.4f}, 95% CI={c_res['ci_95']}, "
                f"p={c_res['p_value_two_sided']:.4f})"
            )

    if factorial_analysis and factorial_analysis.get("complete_2x2x2_lattice"):
        print("\n" + "-" * 114)
        print(" 2^3 FACTORIAL MAIN EFFECTS, INTERACTIONS & EXACT SHAPLEY CREDITS (on -Bargaining_W1)")
        print("-" * 114)
        print(f"  Main Effects:   {factorial_analysis['main_effects']}")
        print(f"  Interactions:   {factorial_analysis['interactions']}")
        print(f"  Exact Shapley:  {factorial_analysis['exact_shapley_values']}")
    print("=" * 114 + "\n")


def main() -> None:
    t_run_start = time.time()
    args = parse_args()

    if args.seeds < 30 and not args.allow_dev_seeds:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Confirmatory clustered inference requires --seeds >= 30 (got {args.seeds}). "
            f"Pass --allow-dev-seeds explicitly for exploratory local debugging."
        )

    with open(args.config, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        raw_cfg = yaml.safe_load(f)
    base_cfg = SimulationConfig(**raw_cfg)

    if args.num_agents is not None:
        base_cfg.num_agents = int(args.num_agents)
    if args.active_ticks is not None:
        base_cfg.active_ticks = int(args.active_ticks)
    if args.burn_in_ticks is not None:
        base_cfg.burn_in_ticks = int(args.burn_in_ticks)

    all_specs = build_full_factorial_and_control_specs()
    cells_arg = args.cells.strip().upper()
    if cells_arg == "ALL":
        selected_cell_ids = [
            "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE",
            "BASE_CLOSED_FORM",
            "C000",
            "C100",
            "C010",
            "C001",
            "C110",
            "C101",
            "C011",
            "C111",
            "CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC",
            "CTRL_SHUFFLED_LAMBDA",
        ]
    elif cells_arg == "FACTORIAL_8":
        selected_cell_ids = [
            "C000",
            "C100",
            "C010",
            "C001",
            "C110",
            "C101",
            "C011",
            "C111",
        ]
    elif cells_arg == "BASELINES":
        selected_cell_ids = ["BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE", "BASE_CLOSED_FORM"]
    else:
        selected_cell_ids = [c.strip() for c in args.cells.split(",") if c.strip()]
        for cid in selected_cell_ids:
            if cid not in all_specs:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Unknown cell_id '{cid}'. Valid options: {sorted(all_specs.keys())}"
                )

    seed_list = [base_cfg.master_seed + m for m in range(args.seeds)]
    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    turn_log_dir = (
        os.path.join(args.output_dir, "turn_logs") if args.save_turn_logs else None
    )

    # Lazily mount the white-box LLM & disjoint RepE artifacts ONCE if any LLM cell is requested
    needs_llm = any(
        all_specs[cid].policy_mode == PolicyMode.LLM_AGENT
        for cid in selected_cell_ids
    )
    shared_model = None
    shared_tokenizer = None
    shared_repe_artifacts = None

    if needs_llm:
        from src.brain.inference_brain import (
            TwoStageRepEController,
            WhiteBoxModelLoader,
        )
        from src.brain.repe_manager import RepEManager

        print(
            f"[BOOT] Mounting White-Box model '{base_cfg.model.model_id}' "
            f"(4bit={base_cfg.model.load_in_4bit}, attn={base_cfg.model.attn_implementation})..."
        )
        # CRITICAL FIX: Utilizing safe fallback logic for MVP runner to prevent token/license crashes
        shared_model, shared_tokenizer = WhiteBoxModelLoader.load_with_fallback(base_cfg)

        boot_repe = RepEManager(config=base_cfg.repe, seed=base_cfg.master_seed)
        shared_repe_artifacts = boot_repe.load_or_extract_artifacts(
            model=shared_model,
            tokenizer=shared_tokenizer,
            model_id=base_cfg.model.model_id,
        )
        print(
            f"[BOOT] Disjoint RepE artifacts ready (Layer={shared_repe_artifacts.target_layer}, "
            f"Probe Test AUROC={shared_repe_artifacts.probe_test_auroc:.4f}, "
            f"Perm p={shared_repe_artifacts.probe_permutation_p:.4f}, "
            f"Cos(w, v)={shared_repe_artifacts.probe_steering_cosine:.4f})."
        )

    # Execute all requested cells across M independent economy seeds
    clusters_by_cell: Dict[str, List[EconomySeedClusterMetrics]] = {}
    for cid in selected_cell_ids:
        cell_spec = all_specs[cid]
        cell_cfg = base_cfg.model_copy(
            deep=True, update={"factorial_cell": cell_spec}
        )
        # Re-validate to synchronize deliberation/memory/repe switches with cell_spec
        cell_cfg = SimulationConfig(**cell_cfg.model_dump())

        print(
            f"\n[EXEC] Cell {cid:<24} | Policy={cell_cfg.policy_mode.value} | "
            f"A(Delib)={cell_cfg.deliberation.enabled} B(MemFric)={cell_cfg.memory_friction.enabled} "
            f"C(RepE)={cell_cfg.repe.mode.value} | Seeds M={len(seed_list)}"
        )

        if cell_cfg.policy_mode != PolicyMode.LLM_AGENT and args.workers > 1:
            # Parallel execution over independent economy seeds for non-LLM baselines
            tasks = [
                (
                    cell_cfg.model_dump(),
                    s_val,
                    not args.skip_windfall_mpc,
                    turn_log_dir,
                )
                for s_val in seed_list
            ]
            seed_results: List[EconomySeedClusterMetrics] = []
            with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as pool:
                futures = [
                    pool.submit(_parallel_non_llm_worker, t_arg) for t_arg in tasks
                ]
                for fut in as_completed(futures):
                    seed_results.append(fut.result())
            seed_results.sort(key=lambda r: r.economy_seed)
            clusters_by_cell[cid] = seed_results
        else:
            # Memory-safe sequential execution on GPU sharing frozen weights & RepE artifacts
            from src.brain.inference_brain import TwoStageRepEController
            from src.brain.repe_manager import RepEManager

            ckpt_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f"checkpoint_{cid}.json")
            cached_by_seed: Dict[int, EconomySeedClusterMetrics] = {}
            if os.path.exists(ckpt_path):
                with open(ckpt_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as cf:
                    for row in json.load(cf):
                        sc_obj = EconomySeedClusterMetrics(**row)
                        cached_by_seed[sc_obj.economy_seed] = sc_obj
                print(
                    f"       [RESUME] Loaded {len(cached_by_seed)} cached seeds from {ckpt_path}"
                )

            seed_results = []
            for s_idx, s_val in enumerate(seed_list, start=1):
                if s_val in cached_by_seed:
                    seed_results.append(cached_by_seed[s_val])
                    continue

                if cell_cfg.policy_mode == PolicyMode.LLM_AGENT:
                    repe_mgr = RepEManager(
                        config=cell_cfg.repe,
                        artifacts=shared_repe_artifacts,
                        seed=s_val,
                    )
                    controller = TwoStageRepEController(
                        config=cell_cfg,
                        model=shared_model,
                        tokenizer=shared_tokenizer,
                        repe_manager=repe_mgr,
                        economy_seed=s_val,
                    )
                else:
                    controller = None

                res = execute_single_economy_seed(
                    cell_cfg=cell_cfg,
                    economy_seed=s_val,
                    llm_controller=controller,
                    eval_windfall_mpc=not args.skip_windfall_mpc,
                    output_dir=turn_log_dir,
                )
                seed_results.append(res)
                cached_by_seed[s_val] = res
                with open(ckpt_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as cf:
                    json.dump(
                        [
                            dataclasses.asdict(cached_by_seed[k])
                            for k in sorted(cached_by_seed.keys())
                        ],
                        cf,
                        indent=2,
                    )

                if s_idx % 3 == 0 or s_idx == len(seed_list):
                    print(
                        f"       Completed {s_idx}/{len(seed_list)} seeds "
                        f"(last seed={s_val}, Valid={res.json_valid_rate:.1%}, "
                        f"Freeze={res.consumable_market_freeze_rate:.1%}, "
                        f"SFC_Err={res.max_sfc_conservation_error:.2e})",
                        flush=True,
                    )
            clusters_by_cell[cid] = seed_results

    # Extract empirical Zero-Intelligence null floor if ZI baseline was run
    zi_barg_floor: Optional[float] = None
    zi_mpc_floor: Optional[float] = None
    if "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE" in clusters_by_cell:
        zi_rep = EconomyMetricsCollector.aggregate_cell_clusters(
            cell_id="BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE",
            seed_clusters=clusters_by_cell["BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE"],
            config=base_cfg,
        )
        zi_barg_floor = float(zi_rep["bargaining_w1"]["debiased_w1"])
        if zi_rep["windfall_mpc_w1"] is not None:
            zi_mpc_floor = float(zi_rep["windfall_mpc_w1"]["debiased_w1"])

    # Aggregate clustered reports for every cell
    cell_reports: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for cid in selected_cell_ids:
        cell_reports[cid] = EconomyMetricsCollector.aggregate_cell_clusters(
            cell_id=cid,
            seed_clusters=clusters_by_cell[cid],
            config=base_cfg,
            zi_bargaining_floor_w1=zi_barg_floor,
            zi_mpc_floor_w1=zi_mpc_floor,
        )

    # Compute Common Random Numbers (CRN) Paired Cluster Contrasts
    paired_contrasts: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    contrast_pairs = [
        ("C111_vs_C000_VanillaLLM", "C111", "C000"),
        ("C111_vs_CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC", "C111", "CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC"),
        ("C111_vs_CTRL_SHUFFLED_LAMBDA", "C111", "CTRL_SHUFFLED_LAMBDA"),
        ("C001_RepEOnly_vs_C000_Vanilla", "C001", "C000"),
        ("C111_vs_BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE", "C111", "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE"),
        ("BASE_CLOSED_FORM_vs_BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE", "BASE_CLOSED_FORM", "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE"),
    ]
    for label, cell_a, cell_b in contrast_pairs:
        if cell_a in cell_reports and cell_b in cell_reports:
            paired_contrasts[label] = clustered_paired_contrast(
                cell_a_by_seed=cell_reports[cell_a]["seed_level_bargaining_w1"],
                cell_b_by_seed=cell_reports[cell_b]["seed_level_bargaining_w1"],
                n_resamples_b=base_cfg.stats.bootstrap_resamples_b,
                seed=base_cfg.master_seed,
            )

    # Compute 2^3 Factorial Effects & Exact Shapley Attribution if all 8 cells ran
    bits_map: Dict[Tuple[int, int, int], float] = {}
    for a in (0, 1):
        for b in (0, 1):
            for c in (0, 1):
                cid = f"C{a}{b}{c}"
                if cid in cell_reports:
                    # Negate W1 distance so positive Shapley / main effects indicate improvement
                    bits_map[(a, b, c)] = -float(
                        cell_reports[cid]["bargaining_w1"]["debiased_w1"]
                    )
    factorial_analysis = (
        compute_2x2x2_factorial_effects_and_shapley(bits_map)
        if len(bits_map) == 8
        else None
    )

    # Persist Provenance Manifest & Full Statistical Evaluation Report
    cfg_sha = hashlib.sha256(base_cfg.model_dump_json().encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
    manifest = {
        "experiment_id": base_cfg.experiment_id,
        "git_commit_sha": _resolve_git_sha(),
        "config_sha256": cfg_sha,
        "selected_cells": selected_cell_ids,
        "n_economy_seeds_m": args.seeds,
        "power_status": (
            "CONFIRMATORY_ADEQUATE" if args.seeds >= 30 else "DEV_UNDERPOWERED"
        ),
        "num_agents_n": base_cfg.num_agents,
        "burn_in_ticks": base_cfg.burn_in_ticks,
        "active_ticks": base_cfg.active_ticks,
        "model_id": base_cfg.model.model_id,
        "wall_clock_seconds": round(time.time() - t_run_start, 2),
        "probe_artifacts_metadata": (
            {
                "target_layer": shared_repe_artifacts.target_layer,
                "probe_test_auroc": shared_repe_artifacts.probe_test_auroc,
                "probe_permutation_p": shared_repe_artifacts.probe_permutation_p,
                "probe_steering_cosine": shared_repe_artifacts.probe_steering_cosine,
            }
            if shared_repe_artifacts is not None
            else None
        ),
    }

    with open(
        os.path.join(args.output_dir, "manifest.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:
        json.dump(manifest, f, indent=2)

    with open(
        os.path.join(args.output_dir, "cluster_metrics.json"),
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8",
    ) as f:
        json.dump(
            {
                cid: [dataclasses.asdict(sc) for sc in sc_list]
                for cid, sc_list in clusters_by_cell.items()
            },
            f,
            indent=2,
        )

    full_report = {
        "manifest": manifest,
        "cell_reports": cell_reports,
        "paired_contrasts": paired_contrasts,
        "factorial_2x2x2_analysis": factorial_analysis,
    }
    report_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "factorial_evaluation_report.json")
    with open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(full_report, f, indent=2)

    _print_report_summary(
        cell_reports=cell_reports,
        factorial_analysis=factorial_analysis,
        paired_contrasts=paired_contrasts,
        m_seeds=args.seeds,
    )
    print(f"[SAVED] Full evaluation report written to: {report_path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# Neuropolis: Endogenous Representation Engineering and Domain-General Cognitive Bottlenecks for Behavioral Alignment of LLM Economic Agents

`RESULTS_STATUS: NOT_EXECUTED` *(Architecture initialized; confirmatory multi-seed runs pending Module A–D implementation)*

---

## 1. Abstract & Scientific Objective

Large Language Model (LLM) agents deployed in multi-agent economic environments routinely exhibit systematic behavioral distortions—excessive RLHF politeness, hyper-rational verbal compliance, immunity to resource scarcity, and hallucinated financial accounting—that diverge sharply from empirical human microeconomic behavior. Prior attempts to mitigate these failures rely either on **prompt priming** (leaking behavioral theory labels directly into system prompts, inducing demand effects) or on **externalized steering heuristics** (using environment-level `if/else` triggers or hardcoded logit overrides that bypass the model's own cognition).

**Neuropolis** introduces a modular, causally identifiable AI control architecture designed to answer a single, falsifiable research question:

> **Primary Research Question:** In a strictly Stock-Flow Consistent (SFC) two-asset household economy cleared via a **Continuous Double Auction (CDA)**, do **Two-Stage Probe-Gated Representation Engineering (Endogenous RepE)** and **Domain-General Cognitive Bottlenecks** (Bounded Deliberation and Episodic Memory Friction) align LLM agent behavior with empirical human behavioral economics curves—without prompt leakage, without external steering triggers, and without rule-based action overrides?

---

## 2. System Architecture

Neuropolis strictly decouples **Environmental Physics** from **Agent Cognition** across four architectural pillars:

```text
========================================================================================================
                 NEUROPOLIS v2: STRICT SFC PHYSICS + ENDOGENOUS COGNITIVE CONTROL
========================================================================================================

  [1. STRICT SFC ENVIRONMENT & CDA ORDER BOOK]
   • Two-Asset Household Ledger: Liquid Cash b_{i,t} (r_b, credit limit b_bar, borrowing wedge kappa)
                               + Illiquid Productive Capital a_{i,t} (r_k > r_b, convex cost chi(d,a))
                               + Consumable Necessity c_{i,t} (metabolic requirement c_min)
   • Continuous Double Auction (CDA): Price-Time Priority Limit Order Book (Bids/Asks, Escrow Lock)
   • Needs Engine: Sub-subsistence consumption degrades physiological Health H_{i,t} in [0, 1]
                                          │
                                          │ Objective Balance Sheet, Order Book Snapshot & Needs State
                                          │ (ZERO Behavioral Theory Labels / ZERO Prompt Leakage)
                                          ▼
  [2. DOMAIN-GENERAL COGNITIVE BOTTLENECKS (Driven by Endogenous Stress S_{i,t})]
   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐   ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   │ BOTTLENECK A: EPISODIC MEMORY FRICTION   │   │ BOTTLENECK B: BOUNDED DELIBERATION BUDGET         │
   │ • Contracts RAG Top-K: K(S) in [K_min,K] │   │ • Truncates `max_tokens` STRICTLY on Stage 1 CoT: │
   │ • Injects retrieval score noise sigma(S) │   │     B_CoT(S_{i,t}) in [B_min, B_max]              │
   │ • Mechanistically models Salience Bias   │   │ • Preserves full budget for Stage 2 Action JSON   │
   └────────────────────┬─────────────────────┘   └─────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────┘
                        └─────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────┘
                                                  ▼
  [3. TWO-STAGE PROBE-GATED RepE (100% ENDOGENOUS STEERING — ZERO EXTERNAL TRIGGERS)]
   ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   │ STAGE 1: UNSTEERED APPRAISAL PASS (Hidden Chain-of-Thought)                                     │
   │  • Model generates private CoT up to B_CoT(S_{i,t}) tokens with ZERO steering hooks active.     │
   │  • Linear Probe reads pooled CoT residual stream h_bar_CoT^(l) at layer l:                      │
   │        lambda_{i,t} = g( w_probe^(l)^T * h_bar_CoT^(l) + b_probe^(l) )   in [0, 1]              │
   │  • Detects emergent internal representation of perceived inequity purely from model cognition.  │
   └──────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                  │ Continuous Scalar lambda_{i,t}
                                                  ▼
   ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   │ STAGE 2: STEERED ACTION GENERATION PASS (Structured JSON Action)                                │
   │  • Conditioned on the exact Stage 1 CoT prefix, model generates the JSON order/portfolio action.│
   │  • Residual hook injects:   h_t^(l) <-- h_t^(l) + lambda_{i,t} * gamma * v_hat_inequity^(l)     │
   │    STRICTLY on Stage 2 Action tokens (never on prompt or Stage 1 CoT tokens).                   │
   └──────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                  │ Structured Order / Capital Action (or ABSTAIN)
                                                  ▼
  [4. ORDER BOOK MATCHING ENGINE & DOUBLE-ENTRY SFC ACCOUNTING AUDIT]
   • Locks collateral in Escrow -> Price-Time Priority Matching -> Settles Sectoral Balance Sheets
   • Enforces Global Monetary & Asset Conservation Error < 1e-9 on every tick

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import numpy as np

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns

from src.evaluation.metrics import (
    HUMAN_BARGAINING_ACCEPT_CURVE,
    HUMAN_BARGAINING_BIN_LABELS,
    HUMAN_BARGAINING_SE,
    HUMAN_HTM_DISTRIBUTION,
    HUMAN_HTM_LABELS,
    HUMAN_MPC_CURVE,
    HUMAN_MPC_SE,
    HUMAN_MPC_TERCILE_LABELS,
)
from src.evaluation.validity_and_stats import (
    compute_2x2x2_factorial_effects_and_shapley,
)


def _apply_publication_style() -> None:
    sns.set_theme(style="whitegrid", context="paper", font_scale=1.15)
    plt.rcParams.update(
        {
            "figure.dpi": 300,
            "savefig.dpi": 300,
            "axes.edgecolor": "#333333",
            "axes.linewidth": 0.9,
            "grid.linestyle": "--",
            "grid.alpha": 0.45,
            "legend.frameon": True,
            "legend.facecolor": "white",
            "legend.edgecolor": "#cccccc",
        }
    )


def _add_watermark_if_synthetic(fig: plt.Figure, is_synthetic: bool) -> None:
    if is_synthetic:
        fig.text(
            0.5,
            0.5,
            "SYNTHETIC - PIPELINE TEST",
            fontsize=34,
            color="red",
            alpha=0.22,
            ha="center",
            va="center",
            rotation=28,
            fontweight="bold",
        )


def plot_fig1_factorial_ablation_and_shapley(
    report: Dict[str, Any],
    out_dir: str,
    is_synthetic: bool = False,
) -> str:
    """
    Figure 1:
      - Left: Debiased 1-Wasserstein Distance (W_1) with 95% Clustered Bootstrap CIs
        across all executed 2^3 factorial cells & matched controls, anchored to the
        Zero-Intelligence Null Floor and Human Meta-Analytic SE Noise Ceiling.
      - Right: Exact 2^3 Factorial Main Effects, Interactions (AB, AC, BC, ABC),
        and Exact Shapley Attribution (phi_A, phi_B, phi_C).
    """
    cell_reports: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = report["cell_reports"]
    factorial: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = report.get("factorial_2x2x2_analysis")

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(13.5, 5.2), gridspec_kw={"width_ratios": [1.35, 1.0]})
    ax_left, ax_right = axes

    cell_ids = list(cell_reports.keys())
    w1_vals = [float(cell_reports[c]["bargaining_w1"]["debiased_w1"]) for c in cell_ids]
    ci_lows = [
        max(0.0, w1_vals[i] - float(cell_reports[c]["bargaining_w1"]["ci_95"][0]))
        for i, c in enumerate(cell_ids)
    ]
    ci_highs = [
        max(0.0, float(cell_reports[c]["bargaining_w1"]["ci_95"][1]) - w1_vals[i])
        for i, c in enumerate(cell_ids)
    ]

    palette = []
    for cid in cell_ids:
        if cid == "C111":
            palette.append("#1b9e77")
        elif cid.startswith("CTRL_"):
            palette.append("#d95f02")
        elif cid.startswith("BASE_"):
            palette.append("#7570b3")
        elif cid == "C000":
            palette.append("#666666")
        else:
            palette.append("#386cb0")

    y_pos = np.arange(len(cell_ids))
    ax_left.barh(
        y_pos,
        w1_vals,
        xerr=[ci_lows, ci_highs],
        color=palette,
        alpha=0.88,
        capsize=3.5,
        edgecolor="#222222",
        linewidth=0.7,
    )
    ax_left.set_yticks(y_pos)
    ax_left.set_yticklabels(cell_ids, fontsize=9.5)
    ax_left.invert_yaxis()
    ax_left.set_xlabel("Debiased 1-Wasserstein Distance $W_1$ (Lower = Closer to Human Curve)")
    ax_left.set_title("(A) $2^3$ Factorial Cells & Matched Controls (95% Clustered CI)")

    # Overlay empirical Human SE noise ceiling and ZI null floor if present
    human_ceil = float(np.mean(HUMAN_BARGAINING_SE))
    ax_left.axvline(
        human_ceil, color="#1b9e77", linestyle=":", linewidth=1.6, label=f"Human SE Ceiling ({human_ceil:.3f})"
    )
    if "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE" in cell_reports:
        zi_w1 = float(cell_reports["BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE"]["bargaining_w1"]["debiased_w1"])
        ax_left.axvline(
            zi_w1, color="#e7298a", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.4, label=f"ZI Null Floor ({zi_w1:.3f})"
        )
    ax_left.legend(loc="lower right", fontsize=8.5)

    # Panel B: 2^3 Main Effects, Interactions & Exact Shapley Attribution
    if factorial and factorial.get("complete_2x2x2_lattice"):
        labels_b = [
            "Shapley: Delib (A)",
            "Shapley: MemFric (B)",
            "Shapley: RepE (C)",
            "Interact: A×B",
            "Interact: A×C",
            "Interact: B×C",
            "Interact: A×B×C",
        ]
        shap = factorial["exact_shapley_values"]
        inter = factorial["interactions"]
        vals_b = [
            float(shap["A_Bounded_Deliberation"]),
            float(shap["B_Memory_Friction"]),
            float(shap["C_Endogenous_RepE"]),
            float(inter["AB"]),
            float(inter["AC"]),
            float(inter["BC"]),
            float(inter["ABC"]),
        ]
        colors_b = ["#1b9e77" if v >= 0 else "#d95f02" for v in vals_b]
        yb = np.arange(len(labels_b))
        ax_right.barh(yb, vals_b, color=colors_b, alpha=0.88, edgecolor="#222222", linewidth=0.7)
        ax_right.axvline(0.0, color="#222222", linewidth=1.0)
        ax_right.set_yticks(yb)
        ax_right.set_yticklabels(labels_b, fontsize=9.5)
        ax_right.invert_yaxis()
        ax_right.set_xlabel("Alignment Gain ($-\\Delta W_1$; Positive = Improvement)")
        ax_right.set_title("(B) Exact $2^3$ Shapley Credit & Interaction Contrasts")
    else:
        ax_right.text(
            0.5,
            0.5,
            "Run --cells FACTORIAL_8 or ALL\nto populate complete 2^3 Shapley & Interactions.",
            ha="center",
            va="center",
            fontsize=10,
            color="#555555",
        )
        ax_right.set_axis_off()

    _add_watermark_if_synthetic(fig, is_synthetic)
    plt.tight_layout()
    out_png = os.path.join(out_dir, "fig1_factorial_2x2x2_ablation.png")
    fig.savefig(out_png, bbox_inches="tight")
    fig.savefig(out_png.replace(".png", ".pdf"), bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    return out_png


def plot_fig2_conditional_bargaining_and_mpc_curves(
    report: Dict[str, Any],
    out_dir: str,
    is_synthetic: bool = False,
) -> str:
    """
    Figure 2:
      - Left: CDA Bargaining / Ultimatum Acceptance Gradient across 4 Markup Bins
        vs. Oosterbeek et al. (2004) Human Curve and +/- SESOI Equivalence Band.
      - Right: Exogenous-Windfall Causal MPC across 3 Liquid-Wealth Terciles
        vs. Jappelli & Pistaferri (2014) Human Curve and +/- SESOI Band.
    """
    cell_reports: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = report["cell_reports"]
    highlight_cells = [
        c
        for c in ("C111", "C000", "CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC", "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE", "BASE_CLOSED_FORM")
        if c in cell_reports
    ]
    if not highlight_cells:
        highlight_cells = list(cell_reports.keys())[:4]

    style_map = {
        "C111": ("#1b9e77", "-o", "Neuropolis Full (C111)"),
        "C000": ("#386cb0", "--s", "Vanilla LLM (C000)"),
        "CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC": ("#d95f02", ":^", "Placebo Vector Control"),
        "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE": ("#7570b3", "-.d", "Zero-Intelligence CDA"),
        "BASE_CLOSED_FORM": ("#666666", "--x", "Closed-Form Benchmark"),
    }

    fig, (ax_barg, ax_mpc) = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(13.2, 5.0))

    # Left Panel: Bargaining Acceptance Curve
    x_b = np.arange(len(HUMAN_BARGAINING_BIN_LABELS))
    h_b = np.asarray(HUMAN_BARGAINING_ACCEPT_CURVE, dtype=np.float64)
    sesoi_b = float(
        cell_reports[highlight_cells[0]]["bargaining_per_bin_tost"][0]["sesoi_margin_delta"]
    )
    ax_barg.fill_between(
        x_b,
        np.clip(h_b - sesoi_b, 0.0, 1.0),
        np.clip(h_b + sesoi_b, 0.0, 1.0),
        color="#222222",
        alpha=0.14,
        label=f"Human SESOI Band ($\\pm${sesoi_b:.2f})",
    )
    ax_barg.plot(
        x_b, h_b, color="#111111", linewidth=2.4, marker="*", markersize=9, label="Human Meta-Analysis (Oosterbeek 2004)"
    )

    for cid in highlight_cells:
        bins = cell_reports[cid]["bargaining_per_bin_tost"]
        means = [float(b["sim_cluster_mean"]) for b in bins]
        err_lo = [max(0.0, means[i] - float(bins[i]["ci_95_lower"])) for i in range(len(bins))]
        err_hi = [max(0.0, float(bins[i]["ci_95_upper"]) - means[i]) for i in range(len(bins))]
        col, fmt, lbl = style_map.get(cid, ("#444444", "-o", cid))
        ax_barg.errorbar(
            x_b, means, yerr=[err_lo, err_hi], fmt=fmt, color=col, linewidth=1.8, capsize=3, label=lbl
        )

    ax_barg.set_xticks(x_b)
    ax_barg.set_xticklabels(["Fair\n(<=5%)", "Moderate\n(5–15%)", "Unfair\n(15–30%)", "Extreme\n(>30%)"])
    ax_barg.set_ylim(-0.02, 1.05)
    ax_barg.set_ylabel("CDA Acceptance / Order Fill Probability")
    ax_barg.set_title("(A) Conditional Bargaining Acceptance Curve")
    ax_barg.legend(loc="lower left", fontsize=8.0)

    # Right Panel: Exogenous-Windfall Causal MPC Curve
    x_m = np.arange(len(HUMAN_MPC_TERCILE_LABELS))
    h_m = np.asarray(HUMAN_MPC_CURVE, dtype=np.float64)
    first_mpc_bins = cell_reports[highlight_cells[0]].get("windfall_mpc_per_bin_tost", [])
    sesoi_m = float(first_mpc_bins[0]["sesoi_margin_delta"]) if first_mpc_bins else 0.06

    ax_mpc.fill_between(
        x_m,
        np.clip(h_m - sesoi_m, 0.0, 1.0),
        np.clip(h_m + sesoi_m, 0.0, 1.0),
        color="#222222",
        alpha=0.14,
        label=f"Human SESOI Band ($\\pm${sesoi_m:.2f})",
    )
    ax_mpc.plot(
        x_m, h_m, color="#111111", linewidth=2.4, marker="*", markersize=9, label="Human Windfall MPC (Jappelli 2014)"
    )

    for cid in highlight_cells:
        bins = cell_reports[cid].get("windfall_mpc_per_bin_tost", [])
        if not bins:
            continue
        means = [float(b["sim_cluster_mean"]) for b in bins]
        err_lo = [max(0.0, means[i] - float(bins[i]["ci_95_lower"])) for i in range(len(bins))]
        err_hi = [max(0.0, float(bins[i]["ci_95_upper"]) - means[i]) for i in range(len(bins))]
        col, fmt, lbl = style_map.get(cid, ("#444444", "-o", cid))
        ax_mpc.errorbar(
            x_m, means, yerr=[err_lo, err_hi], fmt=fmt, color=col, linewidth=1.8, capsize=3, label=lbl
        )

    ax_mpc.set_xticks(x_m)
    ax_mpc.set_xticklabels(["Low Liquidity\n(Tercile 1)", "Mid Liquidity\n(Tercile 2)", "High Liquidity\n(Tercile 3)"])
    ax_mpc.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.00)
    ax_mpc.set_ylabel("Exogenous-Windfall Causal MPC ($\\Delta C / \\Delta Y$)")
    ax_mpc.set_title("(B) Cross-Sectional Windfall MPC Heterogeneity")
    ax_mpc.legend(loc="upper right", fontsize=8.0)

    _add_watermark_if_synthetic(fig, is_synthetic)
    plt.tight_layout()
    out_png = os.path.join(out_dir, "fig2_bargaining_and_mpc_curves.png")
    fig.savefig(out_png, bbox_inches="tight")
    fig.savefig(out_png.replace(".png", ".pdf"), bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    return out_png


def plot_fig3_per_bin_tost_equivalence_forest(
    report: Dict[str, Any],
    out_dir: str,
    target_cell: str = "C111",
    is_synthetic: bool = False,
) -> str:
    """
    Figure 3: Per-Bin TOST (Intersection-Union Test) Equivalence Forest Plot.
    Plots exact (Sim_Cluster_Mean - Human_Ref) with bold 90% Clustered CIs (TOST alpha=0.05)
    and thin 95% Clustered CIs against the shaded a priori [-SESOI, +SESOI] equivalence zone.
    """
    cell_reports = report["cell_reports"]
    if target_cell not in cell_reports:
        target_cell = list(cell_reports.keys())[-1]
    rep = cell_reports[target_cell]

    all_bins: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    all_bins.extend(rep.get("bargaining_per_bin_tost", []))
    all_bins.extend(rep.get("htm_per_bin_tost", []))
    all_bins.extend(rep.get("windfall_mpc_per_bin_tost", []))

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10.5, max(4.8, 0.52 * len(all_bins) + 1.4)))
    y_pos = np.arange(len(all_bins))

    for idx, b in enumerate(all_bins):
        diff = float(b["mean_difference"])
        ref = float(b["human_reference_mean"])
        delta = float(b["sesoi_margin_delta"])
        ci90_lo = float(b["ci_90_lower"]) - ref
        ci90_hi = float(b["ci_90_upper"]) - ref
        ci95_lo = float(b["ci_95_lower"]) - ref
        ci95_hi = float(b["ci_95_upper"]) - ref
        equiv = bool(b["statistically_equivalent"])
        valid_cov = bool(b["bin_coverage_valid"])
        p_adj = b.get("holm_adjusted_p", b["tost_p_max"])

        # Per-row shaded SESOI equivalence box [-delta, +delta]
        ax.barh(idx, 2.0 * delta, left=-delta, height=0.68, color="#b2df8a", alpha=0.35, edgecolor="none")
        ax.plot([-delta, -delta], [idx - 0.34, idx + 0.34], color="#1b9e77", linestyle=":", linewidth=1.0)
        ax.plot([delta, delta], [idx - 0.34, idx + 0.34], color="#1b9e77", linestyle=":", linewidth=1.0)

        col = "#1b9e77" if equiv else ("#e7298a" if not valid_cov else "#d95f02")
        # Thin 95% CI whisker + thick 90% TOST CI bar
        ax.plot([ci95_lo, ci95_hi], [idx, idx], color=col, linewidth=1.3, alpha=0.85)
        ax.plot([ci90_lo, ci90_hi], [idx, idx], color=col, linewidth=4.0, solid_capstyle="round")
        ax.scatter([diff], [idx], color="#111111", s=26, zorder=5)

        tag = "EQUIV" if equiv else ("UNDERFILLED" if not valid_cov else "NON-EQ")
        ax.text(
            max(ci95_hi, delta) + 0.015,
            idx,
            f"p_Holm={p_adj:.3f} [{tag}] (n={b['n_observations_in_bin']})",
            va="center",
            fontsize=8.2,
            color=col,
            fontweight="bold" if equiv else "normal",
        )

    ax.axvline(0.0, color="#222222", linewidth=1.0, linestyle="--")
    y_labels = [
        f"{b['benchmark_name'].split('_')[0]} :: {b['bin_label']}" for b in all_bins
    ]
    ax.set_yticks(y_pos)
    ax.set_yticklabels(y_labels, fontsize=8.8)
    ax.invert_yaxis()
    ax.set_xlabel("Cluster Mean Difference ($\\hat{\\mu}_b - R_{\\mathrm{Human}, b}$) with Bold 90% TOST CI & Thin 95% CI")
    ax.set_title(f"Per-Bin TOST Equivalence Analysis — Condition `{target_cell}` (Shaded = A Priori $\\pm$SESOI Margin)")
    ax.set_xlim(-0.45, 0.65)

    _add_watermark_if_synthetic(fig, is_synthetic)
    plt.tight_layout()
    out_png = os.path.join(out_dir, f"fig3_per_bin_tost_forest_{target_cell}.png")
    fig.savefig(out_png, bbox_inches="tight")
    fig.savefig(out_png.replace(".png", ".pdf"), bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    return out_png


def plot_fig4_two_asset_htm_distribution(
    report: Dict[str, Any],
    out_dir: str,
    is_synthetic: bool = False,
) -> str:
    """
    Figure 4: Stacked Two-Asset Hand-to-Mouth Distribution (Poor-HtM, Wealthy-HtM, Non-HtM)
    compared directly against Kaplan, Violante, & Weidner (2014) US SCF benchmark.
    """
    cell_reports = report["cell_reports"]
    labels = ["Human SCF\n(KVW 2014)"] + list(cell_reports.keys())
    poor_vals = [HUMAN_HTM_DISTRIBUTION[0]]
    wealthy_vals = [HUMAN_HTM_DISTRIBUTION[1]]
    non_vals = [HUMAN_HTM_DISTRIBUTION[2]]

    for cid in cell_reports:
        bins = {b["bin_label"]: float(b["sim_cluster_mean"]) for b in cell_reports[cid]["htm_per_bin_tost"]}
        poor_vals.append(bins.get("Poor_HtM", 0.0))
        wealthy_vals.append(bins.get("Wealthy_HtM", 0.0))
        non_vals.append(bins.get("Non_HtM", 1.0))

    x = np.arange(len(labels))
    p_arr = np.asarray(poor_vals)
    w_arr = np.asarray(wealthy_vals)
    n_arr = np.asarray(non_vals)

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(8.5, 1.05 * len(labels)), 4.8))
    ax.bar(x, p_arr, label="Poor Hand-to-Mouth (P-HtM)", color="#e7298a", alpha=0.88, edgecolor="#222222")
    ax.bar(x, w_arr, bottom=p_arr, label="Wealthy Hand-to-Mouth (W-HtM)", color="#d95f02", alpha=0.88, edgecolor="#222222")
    ax.bar(x, n_arr, bottom=p_arr + w_arr, label="Non-HtM (Liquid Savers)", color="#1b9e77", alpha=0.80, edgecolor="#222222")

    ax.set_xticks(x)
    ax.set_xticklabels(labels, rotation=22, ha="right", fontsize=8.8)
    ax.set_ylim(0.0, 1.08)
    ax.set_ylabel("Steady-State Household Share")
    ax.set_title("Two-Asset Household Liquidity Composition vs. KVW (2014) US SCF Benchmark")
    ax.legend(loc="upper right", ncol=3, fontsize=8.5)

    _add_watermark_if_synthetic(fig, is_synthetic)
    plt.tight_layout()
    out_png = os.path.join(out_dir, "fig4_two_asset_htm_distribution.png")
    fig.savefig(out_png, bbox_inches="tight")
    fig.savefig(out_png.replace(".png", ".pdf"), bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    return out_png


def export_latex_summary_table(report: Dict[str, Any], out_dir: str, is_synthetic: bool) -> str:
    import csv

    status_note = "SYNTHETIC PIPELINE TEST" if is_synthetic else "EXECUTED FROM CLUSTERED ARTIFACTS"
    lines = [
        r"\begin{table*}[htbp]",
        r"\centering",
        rf"\caption{{Neuropolis v2 $2^3$ Factorial Behavioral Economics Benchmark Summary ({status_note})}}",
        r"\resizebox{\textwidth}{!}{%",
        r"\begin{tabular}{lrrrrrrrrr}",
        r"\toprule",
        r"Condition & $M$ & Valid\% & Frz\% & Gini & Spread & W-HtM\% & HtM JSD & Barg. $W_1$ (95\% CI) & MPC Slope \\",
        r"\midrule",
    ]

    csv_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for cid, rep in report["cell_reports"].items():
        w1 = rep["bargaining_w1"]
        jsd = rep["htm_miller_madow_jsd"]["debiased_jsd"]
        whtm_bin = next(
            (b for b in rep.get("htm_per_bin_tost", []) if b["bin_label"] == "Wealthy_HtM"),
            None,
        )
        whtm_pct = (float(whtm_bin["sim_cluster_mean"]) * 100.0) if whtm_bin else 0.0
        gini_v = float(rep.get("wealth_gini", 0.0))
        sprd_v = float(rep.get("mean_bid_ask_spread", 0.0))
        mpc_s = rep.get("windfall_mpc_slope")
        mpc_str = f"{float(mpc_s):+.3f}" if mpc_s is not None else "--"

        safe_cid = cid.replace("_", r"\_")
        lines.append(
            f"{safe_cid} & {rep['n_economy_seeds_m']} & {rep['json_valid_rate']*100:.1f}\\% & "
            f"{rep['market_freeze_rate']*100:.1f}\\% & {gini_v:.3f} & {sprd_v:.2f} & "
            f"{whtm_pct:.1f}\\% & {jsd:.4f} & "
            f"{w1['debiased_w1']:.3f} [{w1['ci_95'][0]:.3f}, {w1['ci_95'][1]:.3f}] & "
            f"{mpc_str} \\\\"
        )
        csv_rows.append(
            {
                "condition": cid,
                "n_seeds_m": rep["n_economy_seeds_m"],
                "json_valid_rate": rep["json_valid_rate"],
                "survival_rate": rep.get("survival_rate", 1.0),
                "market_freeze_rate": rep["market_freeze_rate"],
                "wealth_gini": gini_v,
                "mean_bid_ask_spread": sprd_v,
                "wealthy_htm_pct": round(whtm_pct, 2),
                "htm_debiased_jsd": jsd,
                "bargaining_debiased_w1": w1["debiased_w1"],
                "bargaining_w1_ci95_lo": w1["ci_95"][0],
                "bargaining_w1_ci95_hi": w1["ci_95"][1],
                "windfall_mpc_slope": mpc_s if mpc_s is not None else "",
                "max_sfc_error": rep["max_sfc_conservation_error"],
            }
        )

    lines.extend([r"\bottomrule", r"\end{tabular}%", r"}", r"\end{table*}"])
    out_tex = os.path.join(out_dir, "table_factorial_summary.tex")
    with open(out_tex, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

    if csv_rows:
        out_csv = os.path.join(out_dir, "table_factorial_summary.csv")
        with open(out_csv, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as cf:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(cf, fieldnames=list(csv_rows[0].keys()))
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerows(csv_rows)

    return out_tex

def _build_synthetic_pipeline_test_report() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Strictly for unit-testing the plotting pipeline with visible SYNTHETIC watermark."""
    cells = [
        "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE",
        "C000", "C100", "C010", "C001", "C110", "C101", "C011", "C111",
        "CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC",
    ]
    w1_demo = {
        "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE": 0.28,
        "C000": 0.22, "C100": 0.19, "C010": 0.20, "C001": 0.14,
        "C110": 0.17, "C101": 0.11, "C011": 0.12, "C111": 0.08,
        "CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC": 0.18,
    }
    cell_reports: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for cid in cells:
        w1 = w1_demo[cid]
        shift = 0.02 if cid == "C111" else (0.14 if cid == "BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE" else 0.08)
        barg_bins = []
        for lbl, h_val in zip(HUMAN_BARGAINING_BIN_LABELS, HUMAN_BARGAINING_ACCEPT_CURVE):
            sim_m = round(max(0.02, min(0.98, h_val - shift)), 4)
            diff = round(sim_m - h_val, 4)
            barg_bins.append(
                {
                    "benchmark_name": "Oosterbeek_2004",
                    "bin_label": lbl,
                    "n_clusters_m": 30,
                    "n_observations_in_bin": 120,
                    "bin_coverage_valid": True,
                    "sim_cluster_mean": sim_m,
                    "human_reference_mean": h_val,
                    "mean_difference": diff,
                    "clustered_se": 0.015,
                    "ci_90_lower": round(sim_m - 0.025, 4),
                    "ci_90_upper": round(sim_m + 0.025, 4),
                    "ci_95_lower": round(sim_m - 0.030, 4),
                    "ci_95_upper": round(sim_m + 0.030, 4),
                    "sesoi_margin_delta": 0.08,
                    "tost_p_max": 0.008 if abs(diff) < 0.05 else 0.42,
                    "holm_adjusted_p": 0.016 if abs(diff) < 0.05 else 0.65,
                    "statistically_equivalent": abs(diff) < 0.05,
                }
            )
        htm_bins = [
            {
                "benchmark_name": "KVW_2014_HtM",
                "bin_label": lbl,
                "n_clusters_m": 30,
                "n_observations_in_bin": 90,
                "bin_coverage_valid": True,
                "sim_cluster_mean": h_val,
                "human_reference_mean": h_val,
                "mean_difference": 0.0,
                "clustered_se": 0.01,
                "ci_90_lower": h_val - 0.016,
                "ci_90_upper": h_val + 0.016,
                "ci_95_lower": h_val - 0.020,
                "ci_95_upper": h_val + 0.020,
                "sesoi_margin_delta": 0.05,
                "tost_p_max": 0.002,
                "holm_adjusted_p": 0.006,
                "statistically_equivalent": True,
            }
            for lbl, h_val in zip(HUMAN_HTM_LABELS, HUMAN_HTM_DISTRIBUTION)
        ]
        mpc_bins = [
            {
                "benchmark_name": "Jappelli_2014_MPC",
                "bin_label": lbl,
                "n_clusters_m": 30,
                "n_observations_in_bin": 80,
                "bin_coverage_valid": True,
                "sim_cluster_mean": round(max(0.05, h_val - 0.5 * shift), 4),
                "human_reference_mean": h_val,
                "mean_difference": round(-0.5 * shift, 4),
                "clustered_se": 0.014,
                "ci_90_lower": round(h_val - 0.5 * shift - 0.022, 4),
                "ci_90_upper": round(h_val - 0.5 * shift + 0.022, 4),
                "ci_95_lower": round(h_val - 0.5 * shift - 0.028, 4),
                "ci_95_upper": round(h_val - 0.5 * shift + 0.028, 4),
                "sesoi_margin_delta": 0.06,
                "tost_p_max": 0.012 if abs(0.5 * shift) < 0.03 else 0.50,
                "holm_adjusted_p": 0.024 if abs(0.5 * shift) < 0.03 else 0.70,
                "statistically_equivalent": abs(0.5 * shift) < 0.03,
            }
            for lbl, h_val in zip(HUMAN_MPC_TERCILE_LABELS, HUMAN_MPC_CURVE)
        ]
        cell_reports[cid] = {
            "n_economy_seeds_m": 30,
            "json_valid_rate": 0.98,
            "market_freeze_rate": 0.04,
            "survival_rate": 0.96,
            "max_sfc_conservation_error": 0.0,
            "bargaining_w1": {"debiased_w1": w1, "ci_95": (max(0.0, w1 - 0.02), w1 + 0.02)},
            "bargaining_per_bin_tost": barg_bins,
            "htm_miller_madow_jsd": {"debiased_jsd": round(0.15 * w1, 4)},
            "htm_per_bin_tost": htm_bins,
            "windfall_mpc_per_bin_tost": mpc_bins,
        }

    bits_map = {
        (a, b, c): -w1_demo[f"C{a}{b}{c}"]
        for a in (0, 1)
        for b in (0, 1)
        for c in (0, 1)
    }
    return {
        "cell_reports": cell_reports,
        "factorial_2x2x2_analysis": compute_2x2x2_factorial_effects_and_shapley(bits_map),
    }


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Neuropolis v2 Thesis Publication Figure & LaTeX Table Generator"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--report-json",
        type=str,
        default="results/neuropolis_v2/factorial_evaluation_report.json",
        help="Path to `factorial_evaluation_report.json` generated by `run_mvp.py`.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir",
        type=str,
        default="results/neuropolis_v2/figures",
        help="Directory to write 300-DPI PNG/PDF figures and LaTeX tables.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--target-tost-cell",
        type=str,
        default="C111",
        help="Cell ID to display in the Figure 3 Per-Bin TOST Forest Plot.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--synthetic-pipeline-test",
        action="store_true",
        help="Generate watermarked `SYNTHETIC - PIPELINE TEST` figures strictly to verify plotting code.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    _apply_publication_style()
    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)

    if args.synthetic_pipeline_test:
        report = _build_synthetic_pipeline_test_report()
        is_synth = True
    else:
        if not os.path.exists(args.report_json):
            print("RESULTS_STATUS: NOT_EXECUTED")
            print(
                f"No executed report found at '{args.report_json}'. "
                f"Run `run_mvp.py` first, or pass `--synthetic-pipeline-test` to unit-test plotting."
            )
            return
        with open(args.report_json, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            report = json.load(f)
        is_synth = False

    f1 = plot_fig1_factorial_ablation_and_shapley(report, args.output_dir, is_synthetic=is_synth)
    f2 = plot_fig2_conditional_bargaining_and_mpc_curves(report, args.output_dir, is_synthetic=is_synth)
    f3 = plot_fig3_per_bin_tost_equivalence_forest(
        report, args.output_dir, target_cell=args.target_tost_cell, is_synthetic=is_synth
    )
    f4 = plot_fig4_two_asset_htm_distribution(report, args.output_dir, is_synthetic=is_synth)
    tex = export_latex_summary_table(report, args.output_dir, is_synthetic=is_synth)

    print("[REPORTING] Generated publication artifacts:")
    for path in (f1, f2, f3, f4, tex):
        print(f"  - {path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
FILE_PATH: ./scripts/PREREGISTRATION_SKELETON.md

# Preregistration Protocol: Endogenous Probe-Gated Representation Engineering and Domain-General Cognitive Bottlenecks in a Continuous Double Auction Two-Asset Economy

**Project:** Neuropolis v2  
**Status:** `PREREGISTRATION_DRAFT — PARAMETERS TO BE LOCKED AFTER PILOT GATES G1–G4`  
**Results Status:** `RESULTS_STATUS: NOT_EXECUTED`  
**Target Registry:** Open Science Framework (OSF) / Git Tagged Commit Hash (`TBD_AT_FREEZE`)

---

## 1. Strict Boundary Between Pilot/Calibration Phase and Confirmatory Phase

To prevent Researcher Degrees of Freedom (forking paths), hyperparameter overfitting, and circular tuning on evaluation benchmarks, this study enforces an **airtight, three-way split** across random economy seeds, prompt calibration datasets, and macroeconomic shock regimes.

### 1.1 Disjoint Data & Seed Partitions
| Partition | Seed Range (`economy_seed`) | Macroeconomic Regimes Permitted | Permitted Operations |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Phase I: Pilot & Calibration (`P_cal`)** | `S_cal = {1001, ..., 1010}` ($M_{\text{cal}} = 10$) | `NONE`, `TFP_SUBSISTENCE_CONTRACTION` | Execute Pre-Flight Pilot Gates G1–G4; select target layer $l^*$, probe threshold $\tau^*$, and steering gain $\gamma^*$; verify JSON validity $\ge 98\%$ and CDA liquidity. |
| **Phase II: Confirmatory Sweep (`P_conf`)** | `S_conf = {2001, ..., 2030}` ($M_{\text{conf}} = 30$) | `NONE`, `TFP_SUBSISTENCE_CONTRACTION`, `ASYMMETRIC_WAGE_SHOCK` | **Frozen config only.** Run complete $2^3$ factorial matrix + matched negative controls once. Zero parameter tuning permitted after tag freeze. |
| **Phase III: Held-Out Generalization (`P_hold`)** | `S_hold = {3001, ..., 3030}` ($M_{\text{hold}} = 30$) | `CAPITAL_YIELD_COLLAPSE`, `CREDIT_WEDGE_CRUNCH` | Opened strictly once after Phase II analysis to test out-of-sample regime generalization and secondary model-family replication. |

### 1.2 Freeze & Deviation Protocol
1. **Hard Freeze Gate:** Phase II (`P_conf`) may begin **if and only if** all four Pre-Flight Pilot Gates (`scripts/run_pilot_gates.py`) pass on `P_cal`, the locked parameters $(l^*, \tau^*, \gamma^*, B_{\min}, K_{\min}, \sigma_{\max})$ are recorded in Section 7 of this document, and the repository commit hash is tagged (`git tag prereg-v2-freeze`) and archived on OSF.
2. **Protocol Deviations:** Any modification after the freeze tag (e.g., bug fix, exclusion rule update, or exploratory post-hoc probe) must be logged with a timestamp and rationale in `prereg/deviations.md` and labeled **Exploratory** in all tables and figures.
3. **Disjoint Out-of-Domain Probe Extraction:** The Linear Probe $(\mathbf{w}^{(l^*)}, b^{(l^*)})$ is trained on sterile Out-of-Domain Dataset A (collaborative workplace/academic splits, $N = 120$ matched pairs), and the RepE steering vector $\hat{\mathbf{v}}_{\text{inequity}}^{(l^*)}$ is extracted independently from sterile Out-of-Domain Dataset B (civic/grant/tournament allocations, $N = 120$ matched pairs). Neither dataset shares vocabulary or structure with the Neuropolis Continuous Double Auction (CDA) economy.

---

## 2. Research Questions & Falsifiable Primary Hypotheses

**Primary Research Question:** In a strictly Stock-Flow Consistent (SFC) two-asset household economy cleared via a Continuous Double Auction (CDA) under sanitized, jargon-free framing, do **Two-Stage Endogenous Probe-Gated RepE** and **Domain-General Cognitive Bottlenecks** (Bounded Deliberation Budget and Episodic Memory Friction) align LLM agent response curves with published human empirical benchmarks better than unconstrained prompting and matched mechanistic controls?

| ID | Pre-Registered Hypothesis | Primary Estimand | Statistical Test | Explicit Falsification Criterion |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **H1** | Endogenous Probe-Gated RepE (`C111`) improves alignment with the Oosterbeek et al. (2004) bargaining acceptance curve over unconstrained Vanilla LLM (`C000`) | Paired cluster difference $\Delta W_1 = W_1(\text{C111}) - W_1(\text{C000})$ across $M = 30$ CRN seeds | Paired Clustered Bootstrap ($B=10,000$) & paired Student-$t$ ($df=29$, Holm-Bonferroni) | 95% clustered CI of $\Delta W_1$ includes $0$ or $\Delta W_1 \ge 0$ |
| **H2** | Endogenous RepE alignment gain is direction- and state-specific (not a generic perturbation artifact) | Paired contrasts $W_1(\text{C111}) - W_1(\text{CTRL\_PLACEBO\_VEC})$ and $W_1(\text{C111}) - W_1(\text{CTRL\_SHUFFLED\_LAMBDA})$ | Paired Clustered Bootstrap & paired Student-$t$ (Holm-Bonferroni) | Either 95% CI includes $0$ or either control matches/beats `C111` |
| **H3** | Sign-reversed steering (`CTRL_SIGN_FLIP`, $-\gamma \hat{\mathbf{v}}$) reverses the directional behavioral shift induced by $+\gamma \hat{\mathbf{v}}$ | Mean order acceptance & reservation markup shift under $+\gamma$ vs. $-\gamma$ | Paired Clustered Bootstrap | Behavioral shift fails to invert sign or overlaps with $\gamma = 0$ |
| **H4** | Full architecture (`C111`) achieves Per-Bin TOST statistical equivalence with published human curves (Bargaining, Two-Asset HtM, Windfall MPC) | Per-bin mean difference $\hat{\mu}_b - R_{\text{Human},b}$ against $\pm \Delta_{\text{SESOI}}$ | Per-Bin Intersection-Union TOST with clustered SE (Holm-Bonferroni) | Adjusted $p_{\text{TOST}} \ge 0.05$ or bin count $< 30$ |
| **H5** | Cognitive control components exhibit non-additive synergy across the $2^3$ factorial lattice | Exact $2^3$ interaction contrasts ($\beta_{AB}, \beta_{AC}, \beta_{BC}, \beta_{ABC}$) and Shapley shares ($\phi_A, \phi_B, \phi_C$) | Nested F-test (additive vs. interaction model across $M=30$ seeds) | Interaction terms do not improve fit ($p \ge 0.05$) |
| **H6** | Parameters locked on `P_cal` generalize out-of-sample to held-out shock regimes (`P_hold`) | Paired $\Delta W_1$ on `CAPITAL_YIELD_COLLAPSE` & `CREDIT_WEDGE_CRUNCH` | Paired Clustered Bootstrap on $S_{\text{hold}}$ | Effect sign reverses or 95% CI includes $0$ |

*Commitment to Null Results:* Any hypothesis that meets its falsification criterion will be reported prominently in the main results table as **Not Supported**, alongside exact point estimates and 95% clustered CIs.

---

## 3. Exact $2^3$ Factorial Arms & Isolated Mechanisms

All LLM arms receive identical **Sanitized Framing** prompts (verified at runtime by `assert_prompt_sanitized`, banning all behavioral economics jargon such as *unfair, ultimatum, loan, debt, gouging, scarcity, loss aversion, human, trust*). Steering hooks are strictly forbidden during Stage 1 CoT and strictly restricted to Stage 2 Action tokens, with **zero post-hoc logit modifications** and **zero silent rule fallbacks** (exhausted JSON retries emit explicit `ABSTAIN`).

### 3.1 Isolated Mechanisms
* **Factor A — Bounded Deliberation Budget ($B_{\text{CoT}}$):** Dynamically compresses `max_tokens` **strictly for Stage 1 hidden CoT** via $B_{\text{CoT}}(S_{\text{eff}}) = \lfloor B_{\min} + (B_{\max} - B_{\min})\exp(-k_{\text{delib}} S_{\text{eff}}) \rfloor$, while leaving Stage 2 JSON Action `max_tokens` (`128`) strictly invariant to stress.
* **Factor B — Episodic Memory Friction ($M_{\text{RAG}}$):** Dynamically contracts episodic RAG retrieval depth $K(S) \in [K_{\min}, K_{\max}]$ and injects Gaussian retrieval noise $\sigma(S) = \sigma_{\max} S$ into query embeddings and cosine similarity rankings, mechanistically inducing salience bias and imperfect recall.
* **Factor C — Two-Stage Endogenous Probe-Gated RepE ($R_{\text{RepE}}$):** Stage 1 generates an unsteered hidden CoT; the frozen linear probe $(\mathbf{w}^{(l^*)}, b^{(l^*)})$ computes $\lambda_{i,t} = \sigma\big((\mathbf{w}^{\top}\bar{\mathbf{h}}_{\text{CoT}}^{(l^*)} + b - \tau^*)/T\big) \in [0, 1]$ purely from the model's CoT residual stream; Stage 2 injects $\mathbf{h}' = \mathbf{h} + \lambda_{i,t} \gamma^* \hat{\mathbf{v}}_{\text{inequity}}^{(l^*)}$ strictly into Action tokens (capped at relative residual norm $\le 0.25$).

### 3.2 Complete $2^3$ Factorial Design Matrix
| Cell ID | Factor A ($B_{\text{CoT}}$) | Factor B ($M_{\text{RAG}}$) | Factor C ($R_{\text{RepE}}$) | Experimental Role |
| :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **`C000`** | OFF | OFF | OFF | Unconstrained Sanitized Vanilla LLM Baseline |
| **`C100`** | ON | OFF | OFF | Isolated Main Effect: Bounded Deliberation |
| **`C010`** | OFF | ON | OFF | Isolated Main Effect: Episodic Memory Friction |
| **`C001`** | OFF | OFF | ON | Isolated Main Effect: Two-Stage Endogenous RepE |
| **`C110`** | ON | ON | OFF | Joint Domain-General Cognitive Bottlenecks (No RepE) |
| **`C101`** | ON | OFF | ON | Two-Way Interaction: Deliberation $\times$ RepE |
| **`C011`** | OFF | ON | ON | Two-Way Interaction: Memory Friction $\times$ RepE |
| **`C111`** | ON | ON | ON | Full Neuropolis Cognitive Control Architecture |

---

## 4. Exact Negative & Positive Control Suite (Paired via Common Random Numbers)

Every control condition runs on the exact same `S_conf` economy seeds as `C000`–`C111`:

| Control ID | Exact Specification | Confound Ruled Out |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`CTRL_PLACEBO_VEC`** | Identical to `C111`, replacing $\hat{\mathbf{v}}_{\text{inequity}}$ with a unit-norm random vector $\hat{\mathbf{v}}_{\text{rand}}$ orthogonal to both $\hat{\mathbf{v}}_{\text{inequity}}$ and $\mathbf{w}_{\text{probe}}$, injected with the exact same L2 norm $\lambda \gamma^*$ at layer $l^*$. | Rules out generic activation-perturbation / noise-injection artifacts. |
| **`CTRL_ADJACENT_CONCEPT`** | Identical to `C111`, replacing $\hat{\mathbf{v}}_{\text{inequity}}$ with an orthogonalized adjacent-concept direction (e.g., general lexical urgency/negative-valence extracted from non-distributional texts) matched on L2 norm. | Rules out broad sentiment/valence drift rather than distributional inequity representation. |
| **`CTRL_SIGN_FLIP`** | Identical to `C111`, negating steering gain ($-\gamma^* \hat{\mathbf{v}}_{\text{inequity}}$). | Verifies causal directional specificity (bidirectional dose response). |
| **`CTRL_SHUFFLED_LAMBDA`** | Identical to `C111`, permuting the endogenous gate scalar $\lambda_{i,t}$ across turns/agents within the same tick. | Verifies that *endogenous, state-contingent* gating (not constant background injection) drives the effect. |
| **`BASE_ZERO_INTELLIGENCE`** | Budget-constrained Zero-Intelligence CDA traders (Gode & Sunder, 1993). | Establishes the empirical null floor ($D_{\text{ZI}}$) and proves market freezes stem from cognition, not CDA mechanics. |
| **`BASE_CLOSED_FORM`** | Non-LLM deterministic Stone-Geary / Two-Asset policy operating on the identical CDA order book. | Establishes the non-LLM behavioral reference comparator. |

---

## 5. *A Priori* SESOI Margins & Per-Bin TOST Protocol

Alignment with published empirical human benchmarks is tested **per bin** via the **Intersection-Union Two One-Sided Tests (TOST)** procedure using clustered standard errors over $M$ independent economy seeds ($\text{df} = M - 1$), with family-wise **Holm-Bonferroni** adjustment across all bins within each benchmark family and a strict minimum coverage floor of $n \ge 30$ observations per bin.

| Empirical Benchmark | Published Human Reference & Bins | *A Priori* SESOI Margin ($\pm \Delta_{\text{SESOI}}$) | Justification of SESOI |
| :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **1. CDA Bargaining / Offer Acceptance Curve** | Oosterbeek, Sloof, & van de Kuilen (2004, *Exp. Econ.*) meta-analysis across 4 relative markup bins: `[0.94, 0.76, 0.42, 0.14]` (meta-analytic SEs `[0.03, 0.04, 0.05, 0.04]`) | **$\pm 0.08$** ($\pm 8\text{ pp}$) | Twice the average cross-study meta-analytic SE ($\approx 0.04$), representing the smallest practically distinguishable behavioral shift across cultural replications. |
| **2. Two-Asset Hand-to-Mouth (HtM) Composition** | Kaplan, Violante, & Weidner (2014, *BPEA*) US SCF microdata: `[Poor_HtM=0.12, Wealthy_HtM=0.20, Non_HtM=0.68]` | **$\pm 0.05$** ($\pm 5\text{ pp}$) | Matches empirical cross-survey SCF/PSID sampling variation across business-cycle states. |
| **3. Exogenous-Windfall Causal MPC Heterogeneity** | Jappelli & Pistaferri (2014, *AEJ: Macro*) liquidity terciles: `[Low=0.63, Mid=0.46, High=0.31]` (measured strictly via paired Treasury windfall shadow worlds, zero circularity) | **$\pm 0.06$** | Half of the empirical inter-tercile MPC step ($\approx 0.16$) and $\approx 1.7\times$ empirical survey SE ($0.035$). |

**Distributional Summary Metrics:** Alongside Per-Bin TOST, we report (a) **Bootstrap-Debiased 1-Wasserstein Distance ($W_1$)** with 95% Clustered Bootstrap CIs ($B = 10,000$ resamples over economy seeds) anchored between the empirical ZI Null Floor and Human SE Noise Ceiling, and (b) **Miller-Madow Finite-Sample Debiased Jensen-Shannon Divergence ($\text{JSD}$)** on discrete household liquidity strata.

---

## 6. Statistical Power Justification for $M \ge 30$ Independent Economy Seeds

Because agents interact endogenously inside a shared CDA limit order book, all observations within a single simulation run are correlated; therefore, **the independent statistical unit is strictly the economy seed $m \in \{1, \dots, M\}$** (Clustered Bootstrap and Cluster-Level Student-$t$, $\text{df} = M - 1$).

1. **Paired Confirmatory Contrasts (H1, H2, H6):** Under Common Random Numbers (CRN) across paired seeds, let the standardized paired effect size be $d_z = \Delta / \sigma_{\text{diff}}$. For a two-sided test targeting $1 - \beta = 0.80$ power at Holm-Bonferroni adjusted $\alpha_{\text{adj}} = 0.05 / 4 = 0.0125$ (critical $t_{0.99375, 29} \approx 2.66$), the minimum sample size to detect a medium-to-large paired effect ($d_z = 0.65$) is:
   $$M \ge \left(\frac{t_{1-\alpha_{\text{adj}}/2,\, M-1} + t_{1-\beta,\, M-1}}{d_z}\right)^2 \approx \left(\frac{2.66 + 0.85}{0.65}\right)^2 \approx 29.2 \implies \mathbf{M = 30\text{ economy seeds}}.$$
2. **Per-Bin TOST Equivalence (H4):** For one-sided $\alpha = 0.05$ TOST with $\Delta_{\text{SESOI}} = 0.08$ and expected between-economy cluster standard deviation $\sigma_{\text{cluster}} \approx 0.11$ (verified during Phase I Pilot), achieving $80\%$ power when true $|\mu_b - R_b| \le 0.015$ requires $\text{SE}_{\text{cluster}} = \sigma_{\text{cluster}} / \sqrt{M} \le (0.08 - 0.015) / (1.70 + 0.85) \approx 0.0255 \implies M \ge (0.11 / 0.0255)^2 \approx 18.6$, comfortably satisfied by **$M = 30$ independent seeds** (with option to expand to $M = 50$ if Phase I pilot cluster SD exceeds $0.14$).

---

## 7. Pre-Flight Pilot Go/No-Go Gates & Frozen Parameter Ledger

Before opening `S_conf`, `scripts/run_pilot_gates.py` must pass all four diagnostic gates:

| Gate | Requirement & Pass Criterion | Recorded Pilot Value | Status |
| :---: | :--- | :---: | :---: |
| **Gate 1** | **Probe Transfer & B$\to$C Coupling:** Zero-shot OOD probe AUROC on ~100 sanitized in-game CoTs $\ge 0.70$ (full CoT) and $\ge 0.65$ (taxed CoT); $|\bar{\lambda}_{\text{full}} - \bar{\lambda}_{\text{taxed}}| \le 0.15$. | `TBD` | `NOT_EXECUTED` |
| **Gate 2** | **KV-Cache Faithfulness & Dose-Response:** $\gamma = 0.0$ output 100% bit-identical to `OFF`; monotone shift across $\gamma \in \{0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0\}$ (Spearman $\rho \ge 0.85$); Stage 2 JSON validity $\ge 98.0\%$ across all doses. | `TBD` | `NOT_EXECUTED` |
| **Gate 3** | **Deliberation Bandwidth Sign:** Isolated intertemporal trade-off test comparing `cot_max_tokens=256` vs. `24` verifying whether starvation induces directional myopia vs. noise/RLHF-patience while preserving JSON validity $\ge 95\%$. | `TBD` | `NOT_EXECUTED` |
| **Gate 4** | **Baseline CDA Liquidity & Strict SFC:** Unsteered Baseline + Needs Engine clears CDA without spread deadlock (`market_freeze_rate < 0.25`) and strictly maintains `max_sfc_conservation_error < 1e-9`. | `TBD` | `NOT_EXECUTED` |
