from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass
import math
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterator, List, Optional, Tuple
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from src.core.contracts import (
    ProbeGateMeasurement,
    RepEMode,
    TwoStageRepEConfig,
)


# ============================================================================
# DISJOINT MULTI-DOMAIN OUT-OF-DOMAIN CONTRASTIVE DATASETS (ZERO LEAKAGE)
#   - Dataset A (Probe Training): 3 Disjoint Non-Economic Domains for LODO CV
#       Domain 0: Orbital Relay Telemetry
#       Domain 1: Botanical Greenhouse Resource Dispatch
#       Domain 2: Alpine Expedition Camp Logistics
#   - Dataset B (RepE Steering Vector): Disjoint Deep-Sea Benthic Habitats
# ============================================================================

def build_multidomain_probe_dataset(
    num_pairs: int = 150, seed: int = 101
) -> Tuple[List[Tuple[str, str]], List[int]]:
    """
    Out-of-Domain Dataset A across 3 strictly disjoint non-economic domains
    (Orbital Relays, Botanical Greenhouses, Alpine Camps). Each pair shares
    identical state reserves and differs ONLY in board-quote/log asymmetry,
    enabling Leave-One-Domain-Out (LODO) probe generalization without any
    Neuropolis economic vocabulary.
    Returns `(pairs, domain_ids)`.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    domain_configs = [
        # Domain 0: Orbital Relay Telemetry
        {
            "domain_id": 0,
            "cycle_word": "ORBIT",
            "node_prefix": "Relay",
            "board_prefix": "Docking_Board",
            "log_tag": "O",
            "log_event": "Relay_Fill",
            "vitality_name": "Shield_Index",
            "streak_name": "Low_Shield_Streak",
            "drain_name": "Fixed_Hull_Draw",
            "inflow_name": "Base_Solar_Inflow",
            "triples": [
                ("Station_Kestrel", "ISOTOPE_X", "CORE_CRYSTAL", "Plasma_Cells"),
                ("Beacon_Orion", "CRYO_COOLANT", "SOLAR_MATRIX", "Photon_Units"),
                ("Outpost_Vega", "DEUTERIUM_ROD", "FUSION_COIL", "Ion_Charges"),
                ("Array_Cygnus", "OXYGEN_CELL", "HULL_ALLOY", "Flux_Tokens"),
            ],
        },
        # Domain 1: Botanical Greenhouse Dispatch
        {
            "domain_id": 1,
            "cycle_word": "SOL",
            "node_prefix": "Plot",
            "board_prefix": "Garden_Board",
            "log_tag": "G",
            "log_event": "Canopy_Transfer",
            "vitality_name": "Canopy_Index",
            "streak_name": "Dry_Streak",
            "drain_name": "Fixed_Evap_Draw",
            "inflow_name": "Base_Rain_Inflow",
            "triples": [
                ("Dome_Sequoia", "NITRATE_MIX", "SOLAR_PANE", "Hydro_Drops"),
                ("Trellis_Fern", "HUMUS_PACK", "UV_LAMP", "Mist_Units"),
                ("Arbor_Cedar", "POTASH_GEL", "COMPOST_VAT", "Dew_Counts"),
                ("Glass_Lotus", "MINERAL_VIAL", "DRIP_VALVE", "Stream_Points"),
            ],
        },
        # Domain 2: Alpine Expedition Camp Logistics
        {
            "domain_id": 2,
            "cycle_word": "STAGE",
            "node_prefix": "Camp",
            "board_prefix": "Ridge_Board",
            "log_tag": "A",
            "log_event": "Rope_Transfer",
            "vitality_name": "Warmth_Index",
            "streak_name": "Frost_Streak",
            "drain_name": "Fixed_Fuel_Draw",
            "inflow_name": "Base_Drop_Inflow",
            "triples": [
                ("Summit_K2", "STOVE_CANISTER", "ICE_ANCHOR", "Pack_Points"),
                ("Ledge_Eiger", "DRY_RATION", "DOWN_BIVY", "Sled_Units"),
                ("Col_Matterhorn", "HEAT_PATCH", "CLIMB_WINCH", "Crate_Marks"),
                ("Spur_Denali", "GLACIER_SALT", "SOLAR_TENT", "Trail_Checks"),
            ],
        },
    ]

    asym_logs = [
        "{peer} controlled 92% of {res_a} supply and posted ASK at {hi:.2f} while prior clearing rate remained {base:.2f}.",
        "{peer} held 90% of {res_a} stock and set ASK to {hi:.2f} per unit despite clearing baseline staying {base:.2f}.",
        "{peer} concentrated 89% of {res_a} availability and demanded {hi:.2f} against baseline rate {base:.2f}.",
        "{peer} restricted 94% of {res_a} dispatch and quoted ASK {hi:.2f} versus opposing BID {lo:.2f}.",
    ]
    sym_logs = [
        "{peer} posted balanced {res_a} ASK at {fair:.2f} matching prior clearing rate {base:.2f}.",
        "{peer} offered steady {res_a} stock at {fair:.2f} per unit aligned with clearing baseline {base:.2f}.",
        "{peer} allocated {res_a} availability evenly and quoted {fair:.2f} near baseline rate {base:.2f}.",
        "{peer} kept {res_a} dispatch open and quoted balanced ASK {fair:.2f} alongside BID {fbid:.2f}.",
    ]

    pairs: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    domain_ids: List[int] = []

    for i in range(num_pairs):
        dcfg = domain_configs[i % len(domain_configs)]
        triples = dcfg["triples"]
        peer_name, res_a, res_b, energy_unit = triples[(i // len(domain_configs)) % len(triples)]
        log_idx = (i // (len(domain_configs) * len(triples))) % len(asym_logs)

        cells = round(float(rng.uniform(18.0, 44.0)), 2)
        stock_a = round(float(rng.uniform(0.6, 1.5)), 2)
        stock_b = round(float(rng.uniform(1.0, 3.5)), 2)
        base_rate = round(float(rng.uniform(10.5, 14.5)), 2)
        vit_val = round(float(rng.uniform(0.74, 0.96)), 2)
        streak_val = int(rng.choice([0, 1]))
        vol_val = round(float(rng.uniform(2.0, 10.0)), 1)

        hi_ask = round(base_rate * float(rng.uniform(1.70, 2.15)), 2)
        lo_bid = round(base_rate * float(rng.uniform(0.48, 0.70)), 2)
        fair_ask = round(base_rate * float(rng.uniform(1.00, 1.04)), 2)
        fair_bid = round(base_rate * float(rng.uniform(0.96, 0.99)), 2)

        header_and_state = (
            f"=== {dcfg['cycle_word']} {i + 1:03d} | {dcfg['node_prefix']} Unit_{(i % 12) + 1:02d} ===\n"
            f"STATE:\n"
            f"  {energy_unit}={cells:.2f} (Held=0.00, Floor_Limit=-8.00)\n"
            f"  {res_a}={stock_a:.2f} (Held=0.00, Cycle_Drain=1.0)\n"
            f"  {res_b}={stock_b:.2f} (Held=0.00, Par_Ratio=50.0, Cycle_Yield=0.015, Transfer_Fee=12.80)\n"
            f"  {dcfg['drain_name']}=5.50 | {dcfg['inflow_name']}=14.00 | Base_{res_a}_Output=0.00\n"
            f"  {dcfg['vitality_name']}={vit_val:.2f} | {dcfg['streak_name']}={streak_val}\n"
            f"EXCHANGE_BOARDS:\n"
        )

        pos_s = (
            header_and_state
            + f"{dcfg['board_prefix']}[{res_a}]: Last_Cleared={base_rate:.2f} | Cycle_Vol={vol_val:.1f} | "
            + f"Top_BIDs=[{lo_bid:.2f}x1.0] | Top_ASKs=[{hi_ask:.2f}x8.0, {hi_ask + 2.5:.2f}x6.0]\n"
            + f"RECALLED_CYCLE_LOGS:\n"
            + f"  - [{dcfg['log_tag']}{i:03d}] {dcfg['log_event']}: "
            + asym_logs[log_idx].format(
                peer=peer_name, res_a=res_a, hi=hi_ask, lo=lo_bid, base=base_rate
            )
            + "\nFirst appraise the board quotes and recalled logs relative to prior clearing rates, then analyze your reserves concisely."
        )

        neg_s = (
            header_and_state
            + f"{dcfg['board_prefix']}[{res_a}]: Last_Cleared={base_rate:.2f} | Cycle_Vol={vol_val:.1f} | "
            + f"Top_BIDs=[{fair_bid:.2f}x4.0] | Top_ASKs=[{fair_ask:.2f}x4.0, {fair_ask + 0.25:.2f}x5.0]\n"
            + f"RECALLED_CYCLE_LOGS:\n"
            + f"  - [{dcfg['log_tag']}{i:03d}] {dcfg['log_event']}: "
            + sym_logs[log_idx].format(
                peer=peer_name, res_a=res_a, fair=fair_ask, fbid=fair_bid, base=base_rate
            )
            + "\nFirst appraise the board quotes and recalled logs relative to prior clearing rates, then analyze your reserves concisely."
        )
        pairs.append((pos_s, neg_s))
        domain_ids.append(int(dcfg["domain_id"]))

    order = list(range(len(pairs)))
    rng.shuffle(order)
    return ([pairs[idx] for idx in order], [domain_ids[idx] for idx in order])


def build_disjoint_probe_dataset(
    num_pairs: int = 120, seed: int = 101
) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    """Backward-compatible wrapper returning `(pos, neg)` pairs across all 3 sterile OOD domains."""
    pairs, _ = build_multidomain_probe_dataset(num_pairs=num_pairs, seed=seed)
    return pairs


def build_disjoint_steering_vector_dataset(
    num_pairs: int = 120, seed: int = 202
) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    """
    Out-of-Domain Dataset B (strictly for extracting the RepE steering direction v_hat):
    Uses deep-sea benthic habitat telemetry (100% disjoint from Dataset A's orbital,
    botanical, and alpine domains AND from Neuropolis's CDA economy).
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    habitats = [
        ("Rig_Mariana", "O2_SCRUBBER", "BALLAST_CELL", "Hydro_Units"),
        ("Dome_Pelagic", "THERMAL_BRINE", "TURBINE_PACK", "Depth_Credits"),
        ("Vault_Hadal", "SALINE_FILTER", "PRESSURE_VALVE", "Current_Volts"),
        ("Pod_Benthic", "KELP_NUTRIENT", "SONAR_ARRAY", "Tide_Tokens"),
        ("Bay_Trench", "HELIUM_MIX", "GEO_BATTERY", "Vent_Joules"),
        ("Lab_Abyssal", "PURIFIER_CARTRIDGE", "TITANIUM_SLAB", "Pump_Units"),
    ]
    asym_notes = [
        "{rig} monopolized 93% of {res_a} tanks and quoted {hi:.2f} despite prior clearing rate {base:.2f}.",
        "{rig} withheld 91% of {res_a} supplies and demanded {hi:.2f} per unit over prior clearing rate {base:.2f}.",
        "{rig} captured 89% of {res_a} vents and raised transfer ASK to {hi:.2f} against baseline {base:.2f}.",
        "{rig} restricted 95% of {res_a} valves and posted extreme ASK {hi:.2f} versus BID {lo:.2f}.",
    ]
    sym_notes = [
        "{rig} shared {res_a} tanks evenly and quoted {fair:.2f} matching prior clearing rate {base:.2f}.",
        "{rig} released {res_a} supplies steadily at {fair:.2f} per unit aligned with prior clearing rate {base:.2f}.",
        "{rig} allocated {res_a} vents proportionally at transfer ASK {fair:.2f} near baseline {base:.2f}.",
        "{rig} kept {res_a} valves accessible at balanced ASK {fair:.2f} alongside matching BID {fbid:.2f}.",
    ]

    pairs: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    for i in range(num_pairs):
        rig, res_a, res_b, unit_name = habitats[i % len(habitats)]
        n_idx = (i // len(habitats)) % len(asym_notes)

        units = round(float(rng.uniform(20.0, 46.0)), 2)
        st_a = round(float(rng.uniform(0.6, 1.5)), 2)
        st_b = round(float(rng.uniform(1.0, 3.5)), 2)
        base_r = round(float(rng.uniform(10.0, 15.0)), 2)
        hull_val = round(float(rng.uniform(0.74, 0.96)), 2)
        o2_streak = int(rng.choice([0, 1]))
        d_vol = round(float(rng.uniform(2.0, 10.0)), 1)

        hi_a = round(base_r * float(rng.uniform(1.70, 2.15)), 2)
        lo_b = round(base_r * float(rng.uniform(0.48, 0.70)), 2)
        fair_a = round(base_r * float(rng.uniform(1.00, 1.04)), 2)
        fair_b = round(base_r * float(rng.uniform(0.96, 0.99)), 2)

        prefix = (
            f"=== DIVE {i + 1:03d} | HABITAT Sub_{(i % 12) + 1:02d} ===\n"
            f"STATE:\n"
            f"  {unit_name}={units:.2f} (Held=0.00, Min_Floor=-8.00)\n"
            f"  {res_a}={st_a:.2f} (Held=0.00, Cycle_Drain=1.0)\n"
            f"  {res_b}={st_b:.2f} (Held=0.00, Par_Val=50.0, Cycle_Yield=0.015, Transfer_Fee=12.80)\n"
            f"  Fixed_Hull_Load=5.50 | Base_Vent_Inflow=14.00 | Base_{res_a}_Gen=0.00\n"
            f"  Hull_Integrity={hull_val:.2f} | Low_O2_Streak={o2_streak}\n"
            f"EXCHANGE_BOARDS:\n"
        )

        pos_s = (
            prefix
            + f"Transfer_Board[{res_a}]: Last_Cleared={base_r:.2f} | Cycle_Vol={d_vol:.1f} | "
            + f"Top_BIDs=[{lo_b:.2f}x1.0] | Top_ASKs=[{hi_a:.2f}x8.0, {hi_a + 2.5:.2f}x6.0]\n"
            + f"RECALLED_CYCLE_LOGS:\n"
            + f"  - [D{i:03d}] Dive_Fill: "
            + asym_notes[n_idx].format(rig=rig, res_a=res_a, hi=hi_a, lo=lo_b, base=base_r)
            + "\nFirst appraise the board quotes and recalled logs relative to prior clearing rates, then analyze your reserves concisely."
        )
        neg_s = (
            prefix
            + f"Transfer_Board[{res_a}]: Last_Cleared={base_r:.2f} | Cycle_Vol={d_vol:.1f} | "
            + f"Top_BIDs=[{fair_b:.2f}x4.0] | Top_ASKs=[{fair_a:.2f}x4.0, {fair_a + 0.25:.2f}x5.0]\n"
            + f"RECALLED_CYCLE_LOGS:\n"
            + f"  - [D{i:03d}] Dive_Fill: "
            + sym_notes[n_idx].format(rig=rig, res_a=res_a, fair=fair_a, fbid=fair_b, base=base_r)
            + "\nFirst appraise the board quotes and recalled logs relative to prior clearing rates, then analyze your reserves concisely."
        )
        pairs.append((pos_s, neg_s))

    order = list(range(len(pairs)))
    rng.shuffle(order)
    return [pairs[idx] for idx in order]


@dataclass
class DisjointRepEArtifacts:
    model_id: str
    concept_name: str
    target_layer: int
    hidden_size: int
    probe_weights: torch.Tensor
    probe_bias: float
    steering_vector: torch.Tensor
    placebo_vector: torch.Tensor
    probe_test_auroc: float
    probe_permutation_p: float
    probe_steering_cosine: float
    extracted_from_model: bool


class RepEManager:
    """
    Module C Core (Optimized): Zero-Sync GPU-Cached Batched Two-Stage Probe-Gated RepE.
      - Stage 1: Accumulates the opening `appraisal_window_tokens` residuals strictly on GPU
        for all B agents simultaneously, transferring ONE `[B, hidden_size]` tensor to CPU
        after generation finishes (zero per-token CUDA sync stalls).
      - Stage 2: Pre-moves `v_hat` and per-agent `[B, 1, 1]` magnitudes to GPU once before
        generation; computes norm capping purely in GPU tensor ops with zero `.item()` calls.
      - Fully backward-compatible with single-agent calls (`B=1`) in `scripts/run_pilot_gates.py`.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: TwoStageRepEConfig,
        artifacts: Optional[DisjointRepEArtifacts] = None,
        seed: int = 42,
    ) -> None:
        self.config = config
        self.artifacts = artifacts
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 4099)
        self._recent_lambda_history: List[float] = []

        # Batched Stage 1 pooled residual cache [B, hidden_size] on CPU
        self._batch_pooled_cpu: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self._batch_tokens_inspected: List[int] = []
        # Device-cached steering / placebo tensors keyed by (mode, device, dtype)
        self._device_vec_cache: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], torch.Tensor] = {}

    @staticmethod
    def resolve_effective_layer(target_layer: int, num_layers: int) -> int:
        if target_layer < num_layers:
            return max(0, int(target_layer))
        return max(0, num_layers // 2)

    def _get_cached_device_vector(
        self, active_mode: RepEMode, device: torch.device, dtype: torch.dtype
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        assert self.artifacts is not None
        vec_key = "placebo" if active_mode == RepEMode.PLACEBO_RANDOM_VECTOR else "steering"
        cache_key = (vec_key, str(device), str(dtype))
        cached = self._device_vec_cache.get(cache_key)
        if cached is None:
            src = (
                self.artifacts.placebo_vector
                if vec_key == "placebo"
                else self.artifacts.steering_vector
            )
            cached = src.to(device=device, dtype=dtype).contiguous()
            self._device_vec_cache[cache_key] = cached
        return cached

    def load_or_extract_artifacts(
        self,
        model: Any,
        tokenizer: Any,
        model_id: str,
        force_recompute: bool = False,
    ) -> DisjointRepEArtifacts:
        cfg = model.config
        num_layers = int(getattr(cfg, "num_hidden_layers", 32))
        hidden_size = int(getattr(cfg, "hidden_size", 2048))
        eff_layer = self.resolve_effective_layer(self.config.target_layer, num_layers)
        artifact_path = self.config.probe_artifact_path

        if not force_recompute and os.path.exists(artifact_path):
            try:
                saved = torch.load(artifact_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
                if (
                    isinstance(saved, dict)
                    and saved.get("model_id") == model_id
                    and int(saved.get("hidden_size", -1)) == hidden_size
                    and 0 <= int(saved.get("target_layer", -1)) < num_layers
                    and not torch.isnan(saved["probe_weights"]).any()
                    and not torch.isnan(saved["steering_vector"]).any()
                ):
                    self.artifacts = DisjointRepEArtifacts(
                        model_id=str(saved["model_id"]),
                        concept_name=str(saved["concept_name"]),
                        target_layer=int(saved["target_layer"]),
                        hidden_size=int(saved["hidden_size"]),
                        probe_weights=saved["probe_weights"].float().cpu(),
                        probe_bias=float(saved["probe_bias"]),
                        steering_vector=saved["steering_vector"].float().cpu(),
                        placebo_vector=saved["placebo_vector"].float().cpu(),
                        probe_test_auroc=float(saved["probe_test_auroc"]),
                        probe_permutation_p=float(saved["probe_permutation_p"]),
                        probe_steering_cosine=float(saved["probe_steering_cosine"]),
                        extracted_from_model=bool(saved["extracted_from_model"]),
                    )
                    self._device_vec_cache.clear()
                    return self.artifacts
            except Exception:
                pass

        half_n = max(120, int(self.config.min_contrastive_pairs) // 2)
        probe_pairs = build_disjoint_probe_dataset(num_pairs=half_n, seed=101)
        steer_pairs = build_disjoint_steering_vector_dataset(num_pairs=half_n, seed=202)

        h_probe_pos = self._extract_layer_residuals(
            model, tokenizer, [p[0] for p in probe_pairs], eff_layer
        )
        h_probe_neg = self._extract_layer_residuals(
            model, tokenizer, [p[1] for p in probe_pairs], eff_layer
        )
        w_probe, b_probe, test_auroc, perm_p = self._fit_linear_probe(
            h_pos=h_probe_pos, h_neg=h_probe_neg, seed=101
        )

        h_steer_pos = self._extract_layer_residuals(
            model, tokenizer, [p[0] for p in steer_pairs], eff_layer
        )
        h_steer_neg = self._extract_layer_residuals(
            model, tokenizer, [p[1] for p in steer_pairs], eff_layer
        )
        diff_vec = torch.nan_to_num(h_steer_pos - h_steer_neg, nan=0.0).mean(dim=0)
        steering_vec = F.normalize(diff_vec.float(), p=2, dim=0, eps=1e-6)

        w_unit = F.normalize(w_probe.float(), p=2, dim=0, eps=1e-6)
        gen = torch.Generator(device="cpu").manual_seed(9001 + eff_layer)
        rand_v = torch.randn((hidden_size,), generator=gen, dtype=torch.float32)
        rand_v = rand_v - torch.dot(rand_v, steering_vec) * steering_vec
        rand_v = rand_v - torch.dot(rand_v, w_unit) * w_unit
        placebo_vec = F.normalize(rand_v, p=2, dim=0, eps=1e-6)
        ps_cos = round(float(torch.dot(w_unit, steering_vec).item()), 4)

        artifacts = DisjointRepEArtifacts(
            model_id=model_id,
            concept_name=self.config.concept_name,
            target_layer=eff_layer,
            hidden_size=hidden_size,
            probe_weights=w_probe.cpu(),
            probe_bias=float(b_probe),
            steering_vector=steering_vec.cpu(),
            placebo_vector=placebo_vec.cpu(),
            probe_test_auroc=test_auroc,
            probe_permutation_p=perm_p,
            probe_steering_cosine=ps_cos,
            extracted_from_model=True,
        )
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(artifact_path) or ".", exist_ok=True)
        torch.save(
            {
                "model_id": artifacts.model_id,
                "concept_name": artifacts.concept_name,
                "target_layer": artifacts.target_layer,
                "hidden_size": artifacts.hidden_size,
                "probe_weights": artifacts.probe_weights,
                "probe_bias": artifacts.probe_bias,
                "steering_vector": artifacts.steering_vector,
                "placebo_vector": artifacts.placebo_vector,
                "probe_test_auroc": artifacts.probe_test_auroc,
                "probe_permutation_p": artifacts.probe_permutation_p,
                "probe_steering_cosine": artifacts.probe_steering_cosine,
                "extracted_from_model": True,
            },
            artifact_path,
        )
        self.artifacts = artifacts
        self._device_vec_cache.clear()
        return artifacts

    def _extract_layer_residuals(
        self,
        model: Any,
        tokenizer: Any,
        texts: List[str],
        layer_idx: int,
        batch_size: int = 8,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        device = next(model.parameters()).device
        base_transformer = getattr(model, "model", model)
        layers = getattr(base_transformer, "layers", None)
        if layers is None:
            raise AttributeError("Model architecture does not expose `.model.layers`.")

        if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
            tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
        tokenizer.padding_side = "left"
        target_module = layers[layer_idx]
        captured_batches: List[torch.Tensor] = []

        for start_i in range(0, len(texts), batch_size):
            batch_texts = texts[start_i : start_i + batch_size]
            enc = tokenizer(
                batch_texts,
                return_tensors="pt",
                padding=True,
                truncation=True,
                max_length=320,
                add_special_tokens=True,
            )
            input_ids = enc["input_ids"].to(device)
            attn_mask = enc["attention_mask"].to(device)
            batch_store: List[torch.Tensor] = []

            def _capture_hook(mod: Any, inp: Any, out: Any) -> None:
                h = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
                batch_store.append(h[:, -1, :].detach().to("cpu", dtype=torch.float32))

            handle = target_module.register_forward_hook(_capture_hook)
            try:
                with torch.inference_mode():
                    base_transformer(
                        input_ids=input_ids,
                        attention_mask=attn_mask,
                        use_cache=False,
                    )
            finally:
                handle.remove()
                del input_ids, attn_mask

            if batch_store:
                captured_batches.append(batch_store[0])

        return torch.cat(captured_batches, dim=0)

    @staticmethod
    def _compute_auroc(scores: np.ndarray, labels: np.ndarray) -> float:
        pos = scores[labels == 1]
        neg = scores[labels == 0]
        if len(pos) == 0 or len(neg) == 0:
            return 0.50
        concordant = 0.0
        for p in pos:
            concordant += float(np.sum(p > neg)) + 0.5 * float(np.sum(p == neg))
        return round(concordant / float(len(pos) * len(neg)), 4)

    def _fit_linear_probe(
        self,
        h_pos: torch.Tensor,
        h_neg: torch.Tensor,
        seed: int = 101,
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, float, float, float]:
        n_pairs = min(h_pos.size(0), h_neg.size(0))
        d_model = int(h_pos.size(1))
        n_train = int(0.60 * n_pairs)
        n_val = int(0.20 * n_pairs)

        x_train = torch.cat([h_pos[:n_train], h_neg[:n_train]], dim=0)
        y_train = torch.cat([torch.ones(n_train), torch.zeros(n_train)], dim=0).float()

        x_test = torch.cat(
            [h_pos[n_train + n_val :], h_neg[n_train + n_val :]], dim=0
        )
        y_test = torch.cat(
            [
                torch.ones(n_pairs - n_train - n_val),
                torch.zeros(n_pairs - n_train - n_val),
            ],
            dim=0,
        ).numpy()

        global_mean = x_train.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)
        global_rms = float(
            torch.sqrt(torch.mean((x_train - global_mean) ** 2)).clamp(min=1e-4).item()
        )
        x_tr_norm = (x_train - global_mean) / global_rms

        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        probe_layer = nn.Linear(d_model, 1, bias=True)
        nn.init.zeros_(probe_layer.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(probe_layer.bias)

        opt = torch.optim.AdamW(probe_layer.parameters(), lr=0.03, weight_decay=0.5)
        loss_fn = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()

        for _ in range(180):
            opt.zero_grad()
            logits = probe_layer(x_tr_norm).squeeze(-1)
            loss = loss_fn(logits, y_train)
            loss.backward()
            opt.step()

        w_scaled = probe_layer.weight.detach().squeeze(0).float()
        b_scaled = float(probe_layer.bias.detach().item())
        w_raw = w_scaled / global_rms
        b_raw = float(
            b_scaled - torch.dot(w_scaled, global_mean.squeeze(0)).item() / global_rms
        )

        with torch.no_grad():
            test_scores = (torch.matmul(x_test, w_raw) + b_raw).numpy()
        test_auroc = self._compute_auroc(test_scores, y_test)

        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        exceed = 0
        perm_iters = 200
        for _ in range(perm_iters):
            shuffled_y = rng.permutation(y_test)
            if self._compute_auroc(test_scores, shuffled_y) >= test_auroc:
                exceed += 1
        perm_p = round((exceed + 1.0) / (perm_iters + 1.0), 4)
        return (w_raw, b_raw, test_auroc, perm_p)

    # ========================================================================
    # STAGE 1: ZERO-SYNC GPU-ACCUMULATED BATCHED RESIDUAL CAPTURE
    # ========================================================================

    @contextmanager
    def capture_stage1_cot_residuals(self, model: Any) -> Iterator[None]:
        """
        Accumulates Stage 1 appraisal residuals on GPU across all B agents in the batch
        with ZERO per-token GPU->CPU transfers. Transfers one `[B, hidden_size]` pooled
        tensor to CPU strictly once upon exiting the context manager.
        """
        self._batch_pooled_cpu = None
        self._batch_tokens_inspected = []

        if model is None or self.artifacts is None:
            yield
            return

        base_transformer = getattr(model, "model", model)
        layers = getattr(base_transformer, "layers", None)
        if layers is None:
            yield
            return

        layer_idx = self.resolve_effective_layer(
            self.artifacts.target_layer, len(layers)
        )
        pooling_mode = self.config.probe_pooling
        app_win = max(1, int(self.config.appraisal_window_tokens))

        state: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "prefill_done": False,
            "prefill_last_gpu": None,
            "sum_gpu": None,
            "last_gpu": None,
            "decode_steps": 0,
            "accum_count": 0,
        }

        def _stage1_read_hook(mod: Any, inp: Any, out: Any) -> None:
            h = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
            if not isinstance(h, torch.Tensor) or h.dim() != 3:
                return
            seq_len = int(h.size(1))
            if seq_len > 1 and not state["prefill_done"]:
                state["prefill_done"] = True
                vec = h[:, -1, :].detach().float()
                state["prefill_last_gpu"] = vec
                if pooling_mode == "early_appraisal_cot":
                    state["sum_gpu"] = vec.clone()
                    state["accum_count"] = 1
            else:
                vec = h[:, -1, :].detach().float()
                state["decode_steps"] += 1
                state["last_gpu"] = vec
                if pooling_mode == "early_appraisal_cot":
                    if state["decode_steps"] <= app_win:
                        if state["sum_gpu"] is None:
                            state["sum_gpu"] = vec.clone()
                        else:
                            state["sum_gpu"].add_(vec)
                        state["accum_count"] += 1
                elif pooling_mode == "mean_cot":
                    if state["sum_gpu"] is None:
                        state["sum_gpu"] = vec.clone()
                    else:
                        state["sum_gpu"].add_(vec)
                    state["accum_count"] += 1

        handle = layers[layer_idx].register_forward_hook(_stage1_read_hook)
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            handle.remove()
            if pooling_mode == "last_cot_token" and state["last_gpu"] is not None:
                pooled_gpu = state["last_gpu"]
                cnt = 1
            elif state["sum_gpu"] is not None and state["accum_count"] > 0:
                pooled_gpu = state["sum_gpu"] / float(state["accum_count"])
                cnt = int(state["accum_count"])
            elif state["prefill_last_gpu"] is not None:
                pooled_gpu = state["prefill_last_gpu"]
                cnt = 1
            else:
                pooled_gpu = None
                cnt = 0

            if pooled_gpu is not None:
                self._batch_pooled_cpu = pooled_gpu.to("cpu", dtype=torch.float32)
                bsz = int(self._batch_pooled_cpu.size(0))
                self._batch_tokens_inspected = [cnt] * bsz

    def compute_endogenous_lambda_batch(
        self, batch_size: int
    ) -> List[ProbeGateMeasurement]:
        """Vectorized linear probe scoring across all B agents in the current batch."""
        if (
            self.artifacts is None
            or not self.config.enabled
            or self.config.mode == RepEMode.OFF
            or self._batch_pooled_cpu is None
        ):
            return [
                ProbeGateMeasurement(
                    layer_idx=self.config.target_layer,
                    cot_tokens_inspected=0,
                    raw_probe_score=0.0,
                    endogenous_lambda=0.0,
                    steering_gamma=0.0,
                    injected_vector_l2_norm=0.0,
                    relative_residual_norm_ratio=0.0,
                    repe_mode=RepEMode.OFF if not self.config.enabled else self.config.mode,
                )
                for _ in range(batch_size)
            ]

        h_clean = torch.nan_to_num(self._batch_pooled_cpu.float(), nan=0.0)
        w = self.artifacts.probe_weights.float()
        b = float(self.artifacts.probe_bias)
        raw_scores = (torch.matmul(h_clean, w) + b).tolist()

        theta = float(self.config.probe_threshold_theta)
        temp = max(1e-4, float(self.config.probe_temperature))
        gamma = float(self.config.steering_strength_gamma)
        results: List[ProbeGateMeasurement] = []

        for idx in range(min(batch_size, len(raw_scores))):
            raw_s = float(raw_scores[idx])
            shifted = (raw_s - theta) / temp
            if self.config.gate_activation == "relu_clipped":
                lam = max(0.0, min(1.0, shifted))
            else:
                lam = 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-max(-30.0, min(30.0, shifted))))

            self._recent_lambda_history.append(lam)
            if len(self._recent_lambda_history) > 256:
                self._recent_lambda_history.pop(0)

            eff_lam = lam
            if (
                self.config.mode == RepEMode.SHUFFLED_LAMBDA
                and len(self._recent_lambda_history) >= 2
            ):
                rand_i = int(self.rng.integers(0, len(self._recent_lambda_history)))
                eff_lam = self._recent_lambda_history[rand_i]

            n_insp = (
                self._batch_tokens_inspected[idx]
                if idx < len(self._batch_tokens_inspected)
                else 0
            )
            results.append(
                ProbeGateMeasurement(
                    layer_idx=self.artifacts.target_layer,
                    cot_tokens_inspected=n_insp,
                    raw_probe_score=round(raw_s, 6),
                    endogenous_lambda=round(float(eff_lam), 6),
                    steering_gamma=gamma,
                    injected_vector_l2_norm=0.0,
                    relative_residual_norm_ratio=0.0,
                    repe_mode=self.config.mode,
                )
            )
        return results

    def compute_endogenous_lambda(self) -> ProbeGateMeasurement:
        """Single-agent convenience wrapper (used by `scripts/run_pilot_gates.py`)."""
        return self.compute_endogenous_lambda_batch(1)[0]

    # ========================================================================
    # STAGE 2: ZERO-SYNC GPU-CACHED BATCHED VECTOR INJECTION
    # ========================================================================

    @contextmanager
    def inject_stage2_action_steering_batch(
        self,
        model: Any,
        probe_measurements: List[ProbeGateMeasurement],
    ) -> Iterator[None]:
        """
        Pre-places `v_hat` and per-agent `[B, 1, 1]` magnitudes on GPU ONCE before
        generation. Performs norm-capped latent injection purely on GPU with ZERO
        `.item()` CPU sync stalls inside the forward hook.
        """
        active_mode = self.config.mode
        gamma = float(self.config.steering_strength_gamma)
        raw_mags = [float(pm.endogenous_lambda) * gamma for pm in probe_measurements]

        if (
            model is None
            or self.artifacts is None
            or not self.config.enabled
            or active_mode == RepEMode.OFF
            or not raw_mags
            or all(abs(m) <= 1e-8 for m in raw_mags)
        ):
            yield
            return

        base_transformer = getattr(model, "model", model)
        layers = getattr(base_transformer, "layers", None)
        if layers is None:
            yield
            return

        layer_idx = self.resolve_effective_layer(
            self.artifacts.target_layer, len(layers)
        )
        first_param = next(layers[layer_idx].parameters())
        layer_dev = first_param.device
        max_rel_ratio = float(self.config.max_relative_norm_ratio)

        # Pre-allocate per-agent magnitudes on GPU once [B, 1, 1]
        raw_mags_gpu = torch.tensor(
            raw_mags, device=layer_dev, dtype=torch.float32
        ).view(-1, 1, 1)
        last_stats: Dict[str, Optional[torch.Tensor]] = {
            "capped": None,
            "rel_ratio": None,
        }

        def _stage2_inject_hook(mod: Any, inp: Any, out: Any) -> Any:
            is_tuple = isinstance(out, tuple)
            h = out[0] if is_tuple else out
            if not isinstance(h, torch.Tensor) or h.dim() != 3:
                return out

            bsz, seq_len, hidden_dim = h.shape
            v_dev = self._get_cached_device_vector(active_mode, h.device, h.dtype)
            if v_dev.size(-1) != hidden_dim:
                return out

            mags_b = (
                raw_mags_gpu
                if raw_mags_gpu.size(0) == bsz
                else raw_mags_gpu[:bsz]
            )
            if mags_b.device != h.device:
                mags_b = mags_b.to(h.device)

            target_slice = h[:, -1:, :] if seq_len > 1 else h
            h_norm = target_slice.detach().float().norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-6)
            max_mag = max_rel_ratio * h_norm
            capped_mag = torch.sign(mags_b) * torch.minimum(mags_b.abs(), max_mag)

            last_stats["capped"] = capped_mag.detach().abs().view(-1)
            last_stats["rel_ratio"] = (capped_mag.detach().abs() / h_norm).view(-1)

            delta = capped_mag.to(h.dtype) * v_dev.view(1, 1, -1)
            if seq_len == 1:
                h_mod = h + delta
            else:
                h_mod = h.clone()
                h_mod[:, -1:, :] = h_mod[:, -1:, :] + delta

            return (h_mod,) + out[1:] if is_tuple else h_mod

        handle = layers[layer_idx].register_forward_hook(_stage2_inject_hook)
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            handle.remove()
            if last_stats["capped"] is not None and last_stats["rel_ratio"] is not None:
                cap_list = last_stats["capped"].cpu().tolist()
                rel_list = last_stats["rel_ratio"].cpu().tolist()
                for idx, pm in enumerate(probe_measurements):
                    if idx < len(cap_list):
                        pm.injected_vector_l2_norm = round(float(cap_list[idx]), 6)
                        pm.relative_residual_norm_ratio = round(float(rel_list[idx]), 6)

    @contextmanager
    def inject_stage2_action_steering(
        self,
        model: Any,
        probe_measurement: ProbeGateMeasurement,
    ) -> Iterator[None]:
        """Single-agent convenience wrapper (used by `scripts/run_pilot_gates.py`)."""
        with self.inject_stage2_action_steering_batch(model, [probe_measurement]):
            yield

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
import math

from src.core.contracts import DeliberationBottleneckConfig, NeedsState


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliberationAllocation:
    """
    Domain-general cognitive budget allocated to an agent for a single turn.
      - `cot_max_tokens` is dynamically compressed under high physiological/liquidity stress
        when Factor A (`DeliberationBottleneckConfig.enabled`) is ON.
      - `action_json_max_tokens` is strictly invariant to stress so cognitive narrowing
        never degrades JSON formatting validity.
    """
    cot_max_tokens: int
    action_json_max_tokens: int
    stress_input: float
    health_input: float
    effective_load: float
    bottleneck_enabled: bool
    compression_ratio: float


class DeliberationBudgetController:
    """
    Factor A (Domain-General Cognitive Bottleneck): Bounded Deliberation Budget.
    Replaces lexical token-masking hacks (PASTA) with a topic-blind computational
    bandwidth constraint on Stage 1 internal Chain-of-Thought (CoT) length:

        B_CoT(S_eff) = floor( B_min + (B_max - B_min) * exp(-k_delib * S_eff) )

    where S_eff combines objective physiological/liquidity stress S_{i,t} in [0, 1]
    with acute vitality degradation max(0, 0.50 - H_{i,t}).
    """

    def __init__(self, config: DeliberationBottleneckConfig) -> None:
        self.config = config

    def compute_allocation(self, needs: NeedsState) -> DeliberationAllocation:
        """Computes Stage 1 CoT token budget and stress-invariant Stage 2 JSON budget."""
        stress = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(needs.composite_stress)))
        health = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(needs.health)))

        b_max = int(self.config.cot_max_tokens_calm)
        b_min = int(self.config.cot_min_tokens_stressed)
        action_budget = int(self.config.action_json_max_tokens)

        if not self.config.enabled:
            return DeliberationAllocation(
                cot_max_tokens=b_max,
                action_json_max_tokens=action_budget,
                stress_input=stress,
                health_input=health,
                effective_load=0.0,
                bottleneck_enabled=False,
                compression_ratio=1.0,
            )

        # Objective acute vitality penalty amplifies cognitive load when H < 0.50
        health_load_addon = 0.25 * max(0.0, 0.50 - health) / 0.50
        s_eff = max(0.0, min(1.0, stress + health_load_addon))

        decay = math.exp(-float(self.config.stress_decay_steepness) * s_eff)
        raw_budget = b_min + (b_max - b_min) * decay
        cot_tokens = max(b_min, min(b_max, int(math.floor(raw_budget))))
        comp_ratio = round(cot_tokens / max(1.0, float(b_max)), 4)

        return DeliberationAllocation(
            cot_max_tokens=cot_tokens,
            action_json_max_tokens=action_budget,
            stress_input=round(stress, 6),
            health_input=round(health, 6),
            effective_load=round(s_eff, 6),
            bottleneck_enabled=True,
            compression_ratio=comp_ratio,
        )

from __future__ import annotations

import gc
import hashlib
import os
import time
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

from src.brain.repe_manager import DisjointRepEArtifacts, RepEManager
from src.core.contracts import (
    AgentActionType,
    ModelRuntimeConfig,
    ProbeGateMeasurement,
    SimulationConfig,
    Stage2ActionPayload,
)
from src.orchestration.turn_orchestrator import (
    IsomorphicVocabulary,
    TwoStageGenerationOutput,
)


def has_native_hardware_bf16() -> bool:
    """
    Returns True strictly on Ampere+ GPUs (compute capability >= 8.0).
    Tesla T4 (Turing sm_75) lacks native BF16 tensor cores and must use float16.
    """
    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
        return False
    major, _ = torch.cuda.get_device_capability(0)
    return major >= 8


class WhiteBoxModelLoader:
    """
    Hardware-Optimized White-Box Loader for Llama-3.1 / Qwen-2.5 Instruct models.
      - Uses hardware compute capability (`sm >= 8.0`) so Tesla T4 runs native float16
        while RTX 3050 / A100 / H100 run native bfloat16.
      - Enforces `tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token` and syncs
        `model.config.pad_token_id` (required for batched Llama-3.1 extraction).
      - Provides `load_with_fallback(config)` for automatic Llama-3.1 -> Qwen2.5-3B fallback.
    """

    FALLBACK_MODEL_ID = "Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct"
    FALLBACK_LOCAL_DIR = "./models/Qwen--Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct"
    FALLBACK_TARGET_LAYER = 18

    @staticmethod
    def resolve_local_or_hub_path(model_cfg: ModelRuntimeConfig) -> str:
        # Check if config.json exists directly in local_dir
        if os.path.isdir(model_cfg.local_dir) and os.path.exists(
            os.path.join(model_cfg.local_dir, "config.json")
        ):
            return model_cfg.local_dir
            
        # Check if it exists in a weights subdirectory (for Kaggle compatibility)
        weights_sub = os.path.join(model_cfg.local_dir, "weights")
        if os.path.isdir(weights_sub) and os.path.exists(
            os.path.join(weights_sub, "config.json")
        ):
            return weights_sub
            
        return model_cfg.model_id

    @classmethod
    def load_model_and_tokenizer(
        cls, model_cfg: ModelRuntimeConfig
    ) -> Tuple[Any, Any]:
        source = cls.resolve_local_or_hub_path(model_cfg)
        has_cuda = torch.cuda.is_available()

        tok_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {"trust_remote_code": True}
        if source == model_cfg.model_id:
            tok_kwargs["revision"] = model_cfg.revision
        tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(source, **tok_kwargs)

        if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
            tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
        tokenizer.padding_side = "left"

        if has_cuda:
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
            if model_cfg.compute_dtype == "bfloat16" and has_native_hardware_bf16():
                compute_dtype = torch.bfloat16
            elif model_cfg.compute_dtype == "float32":
                compute_dtype = torch.float32
            else:
                compute_dtype = torch.float16
        else:
            compute_dtype = torch.float32

        load_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "attn_implementation": model_cfg.attn_implementation,
            "low_cpu_mem_usage": True,
            "trust_remote_code": True,
        }
        if source == model_cfg.model_id:
            load_kwargs["revision"] = model_cfg.revision

        if has_cuda and model_cfg.load_in_4bit:
            from transformers import BitsAndBytesConfig

            bnb_cfg = BitsAndBytesConfig(
                load_in_4bit=True,
                bnb_4bit_quant_type=model_cfg.bnb_4bit_quant_type,
                bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=model_cfg.bnb_4bit_use_double_quant,
                bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=compute_dtype,
            )
            load_kwargs["quantization_config"] = bnb_cfg
            load_kwargs["device_map"] = {"": 0}
        elif has_cuda:
            load_kwargs["dtype"] = compute_dtype
            load_kwargs["device_map"] = {"": 0}
        else:
            load_kwargs["dtype"] = torch.float32
            load_kwargs["device_map"] = "cpu"

        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(source, **load_kwargs)
        model.eval()
        for p in model.parameters():
            p.requires_grad = False

        if getattr(model.config, "pad_token_id", None) is None:
            model.config.pad_token_id = tokenizer.pad_token_id

        actual_impl = getattr(
            model.config, "_attn_implementation", model_cfg.attn_implementation
        )
        assert actual_impl == model_cfg.attn_implementation, (
            f"Attention backend mismatch: requested={model_cfg.attn_implementation}, actual={actual_impl}"
        )
        return (model, tokenizer)

    @classmethod
    def load_with_fallback(
        cls, config: SimulationConfig
    ) -> Tuple[Any, Any]:
        try:
            return cls.load_model_and_tokenizer(config.model)
        except Exception as primary_err:
            if config.model.model_id == cls.FALLBACK_MODEL_ID:
                raise
            print(
                f"[LOADER WARN] Primary model '{config.model.model_id}' failed ({primary_err}). "
                f"Falling back automatically to '{cls.FALLBACK_MODEL_ID}'..."
            )
            os.environ.pop("HF_TOKEN", None)
            config.model.model_id = cls.FALLBACK_MODEL_ID
            config.model.local_dir = cls.FALLBACK_LOCAL_DIR
            config.repe.target_layer = cls.FALLBACK_TARGET_LAYER
            config.repe.probe_artifact_path = (
                f"artifacts/probes/inequity_probe_l{cls.FALLBACK_TARGET_LAYER}.pt"
            )
            return cls.load_model_and_tokenizer(config.model)


from transformers import (
    AutoModelForCausalLM,
    AutoTokenizer,
    LogitsProcessor,
    LogitsProcessorList,
)


class PerSampleBudgetLogitsProcessor(LogitsProcessor):
    """
    Enforces exact per-agent Stage 1 Bounded Deliberation Budgets (Factor A)
    inside a single fused GPU batch: once batch element `i` reaches its personal
    `cot_max_tokens[i]`, emits `eos_token_id` strictly for row `i` without
    perturbing any active token logits or blocking calmer agents in the batch.
    """

    def __init__(
        self, prompt_len: int, budgets: List[int], eos_token_id: int
    ) -> None:
        self.prompt_len = int(prompt_len)
        self.budgets = [max(1, int(b)) for b in budgets]
        self.eos_token_id = int(eos_token_id)

    def __call__(
        self, input_ids: torch.LongTensor, scores: torch.FloatTensor
    ) -> torch.FloatTensor:
        gen_step = int(input_ids.shape[1]) - self.prompt_len
        for row_idx, max_toks in enumerate(self.budgets):
            if row_idx < scores.shape[0] and gen_step >= max_toks:
                scores[row_idx, :] = float("-inf")
                scores[row_idx, self.eos_token_id] = 0.0
        return scores


class TwoStageRepEController:
    """
    Optimized Two-Stage Probe-Gated RepE Master Controller.
      - Adds `execute_two_stage_batch()` to run all B living agents per tick in
        ONE Stage-1 CoT GPU batch + ONE Stage-2 Action JSON GPU batch (~15x speedup
        on Tesla T4 while preserving per-agent CoT budgets and per-agent RepE lambdas).
      - Eliminates per-turn `gc.collect()` and `torch.cuda.empty_cache()` stalls.
      - Retains `execute_two_stage_turn()` for 100% compatibility with Pilot Gates G1–G4.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: SimulationConfig,
        model: Any,
        tokenizer: Any,
        repe_manager: RepEManager,
        economy_seed: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.config = config
        self.model = model
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.repe_manager = repe_manager
        self.economy_seed = (
            economy_seed if economy_seed is not None else config.master_seed
        )

    @classmethod
    def from_config(
        cls,
        config: SimulationConfig,
        economy_seed: Optional[int] = None,
        force_recompute_probe: bool = False,
    ) -> "TwoStageRepEController":
        model, tokenizer = WhiteBoxModelLoader.load_with_fallback(config)
        repe_mgr = RepEManager(
            config=config.repe,
            seed=economy_seed if economy_seed is not None else config.master_seed,
        )
        repe_mgr.load_or_extract_artifacts(
            model=model,
            tokenizer=tokenizer,
            model_id=config.model.model_id,
            force_recompute=force_recompute_probe,
        )
        return cls(
            config=config,
            model=model,
            tokenizer=tokenizer,
            repe_manager=repe_mgr,
            economy_seed=economy_seed,
        )

    def _seed_torch_for_call(
        self, tick: int, agent_id: str, stage: str, retry_idx: int
    ) -> None:
        key = f"{self.economy_seed}::{tick}::{agent_id}::{stage}::{retry_idx}"
        digest = hashlib.sha256(key.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        sub_seed = int(digest[:8], 16) & 0x7FFFFFFF
        torch.manual_seed(sub_seed)
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(sub_seed)

    def _format_chat_prompt(
        self,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_messages: List[Dict[str, str]],
    ) -> str:
        messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system_prompt}] + user_messages
        if hasattr(self.tokenizer, "apply_chat_template"):
            try:
                return str(
                    self.tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
                        messages,
                        tokenize=False,
                        add_generation_prompt=True,
                    )
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
        parts = [f"System: {system_prompt}"]
        for m in user_messages:
            parts.append(f"{m['role'].capitalize()}: {m['content']}")
        parts.append("Assistant:")
        return "\n".join(parts)

    def _run_generate_batch(
        self,
        prompt_texts: List[str],
        max_new_tokens_per_item: List[int],
        temperature: float,
        top_p: float,
    ) -> List[Tuple[str, int, bool]]:
        """
        Fused GPU batched generation with per-sample `max_new_tokens` enforcement
        and zero per-turn `torch.cuda.empty_cache()` synchronization stalls.
        """
        device = next(self.model.parameters()).device
        self.tokenizer.padding_side = "left"
        eos_id = self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
        pad_id = (
            self.tokenizer.pad_token_id
            if self.tokenizer.pad_token_id is not None
            else eos_id
        )

        enc = self.tokenizer(
            prompt_texts,
            return_tensors="pt",
            padding=True,
            truncation=True,
            max_length=1024,
            add_special_tokens=True,
        )
        input_ids = enc["input_ids"].to(device)
        attention_mask = enc["attention_mask"].to(device)
        prompt_len = int(input_ids.size(1))
        global_max_new = max(1, max(max_new_tokens_per_item))

        do_sample = temperature > 1e-5
        gen_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "input_ids": input_ids,
            "attention_mask": attention_mask,
            "max_new_tokens": global_max_new,
            "do_sample": do_sample,
            "use_cache": True,
            "pad_token_id": pad_id if pad_id is not None else eos_id,
            "eos_token_id": eos_id,
        }
        if do_sample:
            gen_kwargs["temperature"] = float(temperature)
            gen_kwargs["top_p"] = float(top_p)
        else:
            gen_kwargs["temperature"] = None
            gen_kwargs["top_p"] = None
            gen_kwargs["top_k"] = None
            gen_kwargs["repetition_penalty"] = 1.0

        # Attach per-sample CoT budget enforcer only when batch items have differing budgets
        if (
            len(prompt_texts) > 1
            and eos_id is not None
            and len(set(max_new_tokens_per_item)) > 1
        ):
            gen_kwargs["logits_processor"] = LogitsProcessorList(
                [
                    PerSampleBudgetLogitsProcessor(
                        prompt_len=prompt_len,
                        budgets=max_new_tokens_per_item,
                        eos_token_id=int(eos_id),
                    )
                ]
            )

        with torch.inference_mode():
            out_ids = self.model.generate(**gen_kwargs)

        new_tokens_batch = out_ids[:, prompt_len:].detach().cpu().tolist()
        del input_ids, attention_mask, out_ids

        outputs: List[Tuple[str, int, bool]] = []
        stop_ids = {t for t in (eos_id, pad_id) if t is not None}
        for idx, tok_seq in enumerate(new_tokens_batch):
            budget_i = int(max_new_tokens_per_item[idx])
            # Trim tokens after first EOS / PAD
            trimmed: List[int] = []
            hit_eos = False
            for tid in tok_seq[:budget_i]:
                if tid in stop_ids:
                    hit_eos = True
                    break
                trimmed.append(tid)
            num_gen = len(trimmed)
            hit_limit = (num_gen >= budget_i) and (not hit_eos)
            decoded = self.tokenizer.decode(
                trimmed, skip_special_tokens=True
            ).strip()
            outputs.append((decoded, num_gen, hit_limit))
        return outputs

    def _run_generate(
        self,
        prompt_text: str,
        max_new_tokens: int,
        temperature: float,
        top_p: float,
    ) -> Tuple[str, int, bool]:
        """Single-prompt wrapper (used by `scripts/run_pilot_gates.py`)."""
        return self._run_generate_batch(
            prompt_texts=[prompt_text],
            max_new_tokens_per_item=[max_new_tokens],
            temperature=temperature,
            top_p=top_p,
        )[0]

    def execute_two_stage_batch(
        self,
        stage1_system_prompts: List[str],
        stage1_user_prompts: List[str],
        stage2_instruction: str,
        cot_max_tokens_list: List[int],
        action_max_tokens: int,
        agent_ids: List[str],
        tick: int,
        parse_action_fn: Callable[
            [str], Tuple[Stage2ActionPayload, bool, Optional[str]]
        ] = IsomorphicVocabulary.parse_sanitized_action_json,
    ) -> List[TwoStageGenerationOutput]:
        """
        Executes Stage 1 (Unsteered Appraisal CoT + Vectorized Linear Probe) and
        Stage 2 (Per-Agent Steered JSON Action) across all B agents in two single
        batched GPU forward passes, retrying ONLY invalid JSON indices.
        """
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        bsz = len(agent_ids)
        base_temp = float(self.config.model.temperature)
        top_p = float(self.config.model.top_p)
        max_retries = max(1, int(self.config.model.max_json_retries))

        # PASS 1: Batched Unsteered Stage 1 CoT + Zero-Sync GPU Residual Capture
        stage1_prompts = [
            self._format_chat_prompt(
                system_prompt=stage1_system_prompts[i],
                user_messages=[{"role": "user", "content": stage1_user_prompts[i]}],
            )
            for i in range(bsz)
        ]
        self._seed_torch_for_call(tick, "_".join(agent_ids[:3]), "stage1_batch", 0)

        with self.repe_manager.capture_stage1_cot_residuals(self.model):
            s1_outputs = self._run_generate_batch(
                prompt_texts=stage1_prompts,
                max_new_tokens_per_item=cot_max_tokens_list,
                temperature=base_temp,
                top_p=top_p,
            )

        probe_measurements = self.repe_manager.compute_endogenous_lambda_batch(bsz)

        # PASS 2: Batched Steered Stage 2 JSON Action (retry ONLY failed indices)
        stage2_prompts = [
            self._format_chat_prompt(
                system_prompt=stage1_system_prompts[i],
                user_messages=[
                    {"role": "user", "content": stage1_user_prompts[i]},
                    {"role": "assistant", "content": s1_outputs[i][0] or "Evaluating reserves."},
                    {"role": "user", "content": stage2_instruction},
                ],
            )
            for i in range(bsz)
        ]

        final_actions: List[Optional[Stage2ActionPayload]] = [None] * bsz
        final_raws: List[str] = [""] * bsz
        final_valids: List[bool] = [False] * bsz
        final_retries: List[int] = [max_retries] * bsz
        final_act_toks: List[int] = [0] * bsz

        pending_indices = list(range(bsz))
        for retry_idx in range(max_retries):
            if not pending_indices:
                break
            self._seed_torch_for_call(
                tick, "_".join(agent_ids[i] for i in pending_indices[:3]), "stage2_batch", retry_idx
            )
            retry_temp = base_temp if retry_idx == 0 else max(0.25, base_temp * 0.75)
            sub_prompts = [stage2_prompts[i] for i in pending_indices]
            sub_pms = [probe_measurements[i] for i in pending_indices]
            sub_budgets = [int(action_max_tokens)] * len(pending_indices)

            with self.repe_manager.inject_stage2_action_steering_batch(
                model=self.model, probe_measurements=sub_pms
            ):
                s2_sub_outs = self._run_generate_batch(
                    prompt_texts=sub_prompts,
                    max_new_tokens_per_item=sub_budgets,
                    temperature=retry_temp,
                    top_p=top_p,
                )

            next_pending: List[int] = []
            for local_i, orig_i in enumerate(pending_indices):
                raw_json, act_cnt, _ = s2_sub_outs[local_i]
                final_raws[orig_i] = raw_json
                final_act_toks[orig_i] = act_cnt
                parsed, ok, _ = parse_action_fn(raw_json)
                if ok:
                    final_actions[orig_i] = parsed
                    final_valids[orig_i] = True
                    final_retries[orig_i] = retry_idx
                else:
                    next_pending.append(orig_i)
            pending_indices = next_pending

        per_agent_ms = round(((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0) / max(1, bsz), 2)
        results: List[TwoStageGenerationOutput] = []
        for i in range(bsz):
            cot_txt, cot_cnt, cot_trunc = s1_outputs[i]
            is_ok = final_valids[i]
            payload = (
                final_actions[i]
                if (is_ok and final_actions[i] is not None)
                else Stage2ActionPayload(action_type=AgentActionType.ABSTAIN)
            )
            results.append(
                TwoStageGenerationOutput(
                    stage1_cot_text=cot_txt,
                    cot_tokens_generated=cot_cnt,
                    cot_truncated_by_bottleneck=cot_trunc,
                    probe_measurement=probe_measurements[i],
                    stage2_raw_json=final_raws[i],
                    stage2_action=payload,
                    json_valid=is_ok,
                    abstain=not is_ok,
                    retries_used=final_retries[i],
                    action_tokens_generated=final_act_toks[i],
                    latency_ms=per_agent_ms,
                )
            )
        return results

    def execute_two_stage_turn(
        self,
        stage1_system_prompt: str,
        stage1_user_prompt: str,
        stage2_instruction: str,
        cot_max_tokens: int,
        action_max_tokens: int,
        agent_id: str,
        tick: int,
        parse_action_fn: Callable[
            [str], Tuple[Stage2ActionPayload, bool, Optional[str]]
        ] = IsomorphicVocabulary.parse_sanitized_action_json,
    ) -> TwoStageGenerationOutput:
        """Single-agent wrapper delegating cleanly to `execute_two_stage_batch`."""
        return self.execute_two_stage_batch(
            stage1_system_prompts=[stage1_system_prompt],
            stage1_user_prompts=[stage1_user_prompt],
            stage2_instruction=stage2_instruction,
            cot_max_tokens_list=[cot_max_tokens],
            action_max_tokens=action_max_tokens,
            agent_ids=[agent_id],
            tick=tick,
            parse_action_fn=parse_action_fn,
        )[0]

================================================================================
FILE_PATH: ./src/brain/memory_tms.py
================================================================================

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import json
import math
import sqlite3
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import uuid
import numpy as np

from src.core.contracts import (
    EpisodicMemoryRecord,
    MemoryFrictionConfig,
)


class EpistemicIsolationError(PermissionError):
    """Raised whenever an agent attempts to read or mutate another agent's private episodic memory."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalResult:
    """Audit output of a single stress-modulated episodic RAG retrieval."""
    agent_id: str
    records: List[EpisodicMemoryRecord]
    top_k_used: int
    noise_sigma_used: float
    friction_enabled: bool
    total_candidate_pool: int


class DeterministicHashEmbedder:
    """
    Zero-VRAM, deterministic L2-normalized character/word trigram projection embedder
    in R^{embedding_dim}. Ensures identical semantic retrieval across CPU CI and 4 GB
    GPU workstations without competing for CUDA VRAM with the white-box LLM.
    Optionally wraps SentenceTransformer on CPU if explicitly requested and installed.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        embedding_dim: int = 384,
        model_name: str = "BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5",
        use_neural_backend: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        self.embedding_dim = int(embedding_dim)
        self.model_name = model_name
        self._neural_model: Any = None
        if use_neural_backend:
            try:
                from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

                self._neural_model = SentenceTransformer(model_name, device="cpu")
            except Exception:
                self._neural_model = None

    def embed(self, text: str) -> np.ndarray:
        if self._neural_model is not None:
            vec = self._neural_model.encode(
                text, normalize_embeddings=True, show_progress_bar=False
            )
            arr = np.asarray(vec, dtype=np.float32)
            if arr.shape[0] == self.embedding_dim:
                return arr

        vec = np.zeros((self.embedding_dim,), dtype=np.float32)
        cleaned = (text or "").lower().strip()
        if not cleaned:
            return vec

        tokens = cleaned.split()
        features: List[str] = list(tokens)
        for tok in tokens:
            if len(tok) >= 3:
                for i in range(len(tok) - 2):
                    features.append(tok[i : i + 3])
        for i in range(len(tokens) - 1):
            features.append(f"{tokens[i]}_{tokens[i + 1]}")

        for feat in features:
            digest = hashlib.blake2b(feat.encode("utf-8"), digest_size=8).digest()
            h_val = int.from_bytes(digest, byteorder="little", signed=False)
            bucket = h_val % self.embedding_dim
            sign = 1.0 if ((h_val >> 63) & 1) == 0 else -1.0
            vec[bucket] += sign

        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(vec))
        if norm > 1e-8:
            vec = vec / norm
        return vec


class ScopedMemoryHandle:
    """Strictly isolates an agent's read/write operations to `owner_id`."""

    def __init__(self, store: "EpisodicMemoryTMS", owner_id: str) -> None:
        self._store = store
        self.owner_id = owner_id

    def _assert_owner(self, requested_agent_id: str) -> None:
        if requested_agent_id != self.owner_id:
            raise EpistemicIsolationError(
                f"Epistemic Isolation Violation: Handle '{self.owner_id}' cannot access '{requested_agent_id}'."
            )

    def write_episode(
        self,
        agent_id: str,
        tick: int,
        event_type: str,
        summary_text: str,
        salience_score: float = 1.0,
    ) -> EpisodicMemoryRecord:
        self._assert_owner(agent_id)
        return self._store._insert_episode(
            agent_id=self.owner_id,
            tick=tick,
            event_type=event_type,
            summary_text=summary_text,
            salience_score=salience_score,
        )

    def retrieve(
        self,
        agent_id: str,
        query_text: str,
        current_tick: int,
        stress_index: float,
        rng: np.random.Generator,
    ) -> RetrievalResult:
        self._assert_owner(agent_id)
        return self._store._retrieve_with_friction(
            agent_id=self.owner_id,
            query_text=query_text,
            current_tick=current_tick,
            stress_index=stress_index,
            rng=rng,
        )

    def update_peer_reliability(
        self,
        agent_id: str,
        peer_id: str,
        completed_signal: float,
        tick: int,
    ) -> float:
        self._assert_owner(agent_id)
        return self._store._update_peer_directory(
            owner_id=self.owner_id,
            peer_id=peer_id,
            completed_signal=completed_signal,
            tick=tick,
        )

    def get_top_peers_summary(
        self, agent_id: str, max_peers: int = 3
    ) -> List[Tuple[str, float, int]]:
        self._assert_owner(agent_id)
        return self._store._get_peer_directory_summary(
            owner_id=self.owner_id, max_peers=max_peers
        )


class EpisodicMemoryTMS:
    """
    Factor B (Optimized): Episodic Memory Friction & TMS Store with Vectorized
    In-Memory NumPy Cache and Hard 15-Tick Context Truncation.
    """

    MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS = 110
    MAX_RETENTION_TICKS = 15

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: MemoryFrictionConfig,
        db_path: str = ":memory:",
        use_neural_embeddings: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        self.config = config
        self.db_path = db_path
        self.embedder = DeterministicHashEmbedder(
            embedding_dim=config.embedding_dim,
            model_name=config.embedding_model_name,
            use_neural_backend=use_neural_embeddings,
        )
        self._shared_conn: Optional[sqlite3.Connection] = (
            sqlite3.connect(":memory:") if db_path == ":memory:" else None
        )
        if self._shared_conn is not None:
            self._shared_conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        # Fast vectorized cache: agent_id -> list of (record, float32[dim])
        self._mem_cache: Dict[str, List[Tuple[EpisodicMemoryRecord, np.ndarray]]] = {}
        self._init_schema()

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        if self._shared_conn is not None:
            return self._shared_conn
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;")
        return conn

    def _close_if_not_shared(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
        if self._shared_conn is None:
            conn.close()

    def _init_schema(self) -> None:
        conn = self._connect()
        try:
            conn.executescript(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS episodic_memory (
                    mem_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    tick INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    summary_text TEXT NOT NULL,
                    salience_score REAL NOT NULL,
                    embedding_json TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ep_agent_tick
                    ON episodic_memory (agent_id, tick DESC);

                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tms_peer_directory (
                    owner_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    peer_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    alpha_obs REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 2.0,
                    beta_obs REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 2.0,
                    interaction_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                    last_tick INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                    PRIMARY KEY (owner_id, peer_id)
                );
                """
            )
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            self._close_if_not_shared(conn)

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo: Dict[int, Any]) -> "EpisodicMemoryTMS":
        cls = self.__class__
        new_obj = cls.__new__(cls)
        memo[id(self)] = new_obj

        new_obj.config = copy.deepcopy(self.config, memo)
        new_obj.db_path = ":memory:"
        new_obj.embedder = self.embedder
        new_obj._mem_cache = {
            aid: [(rec.model_copy(), emb.copy()) for rec, emb in entries]
            for aid, entries in self._mem_cache.items()
        }

        src_conn = self._connect()
        try:
            src_conn.commit()
            dump_sql = "\n".join(src_conn.iterdump())
        finally:
            self._close_if_not_shared(src_conn)

        new_conn = sqlite3.connect(":memory:")
        new_conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        new_conn.executescript(dump_sql)
        new_conn.commit()
        new_obj._shared_conn = new_conn
        return new_obj

    def get_scoped_handle(self, owner_id: str) -> ScopedMemoryHandle:
        return ScopedMemoryHandle(self, owner_id)

    def compute_effective_top_k_and_sigma(
        self, stress_index: float
    ) -> Tuple[int, float]:
        s = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(stress_index)))
        # Cap effective calm Top-K at 5 so prompt context remains strictly bounded across 15 ticks
        k_max = min(5, int(self.config.top_k_calm))
        k_min = min(k_max, int(self.config.top_k_stressed))
        if not self.config.enabled:
            return (k_max, 0.0)
        eff_k = max(k_min, int(math.floor(k_max - (k_max - k_min) * s)))
        eff_sigma = round(float(self.config.similarity_noise_sigma_max) * s, 6)
        return (eff_k, eff_sigma)

    def _insert_episode(
        self,
        agent_id: str,
        tick: int,
        event_type: str,
        summary_text: str,
        salience_score: float = 1.0,
    ) -> EpisodicMemoryRecord:
        safe_summary = summary_text.strip()[: self.MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS]
        emb = self.embedder.embed(safe_summary)
        mem_id = f"ep_{tick:04d}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        sal = max(0.05, float(salience_score))

        record = EpisodicMemoryRecord(
            mem_id=mem_id,
            agent_id=agent_id,
            tick=tick,
            event_type=event_type,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            summary_text=safe_summary,
            salience_score=sal,
            embedding=None,
        )

        # Update vectorized in-memory cache with strict 15-tick & 48-record rolling cap
        cap = min(48, int(self.config.max_episodic_capacity))
        bucket = self._mem_cache.setdefault(agent_id, [])
        bucket.append((record, emb))
        min_keep_tick = tick - self.MAX_RETENTION_TICKS
        if len(bucket) > cap or (bucket and bucket[0][0].tick < min_keep_tick):
            bucket = [
                (r, e) for r, e in bucket if r.tick >= min_keep_tick
            ][-cap:]
            self._mem_cache[agent_id] = bucket

        # Persist summary to SQLite (skip serializing 384 floats as JSON when in-memory cache is active)
        conn = self._connect()
        try:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO episodic_memory (
                    mem_id, agent_id, tick, event_type, summary_text, salience_score, embedding_json
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);
                """,
                (
                    record.mem_id,
                    record.agent_id,
                    record.tick,
                    record.event_type,
                    record.summary_text,
                    record.salience_score,
                    "[]",
                ),
            )
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            self._close_if_not_shared(conn)
        return record

    def _retrieve_with_friction(
        self,
        agent_id: str,
        query_text: str,
        current_tick: int,
        stress_index: float,
        rng: np.random.Generator,
    ) -> RetrievalResult:
        eff_k, eff_sigma = self.compute_effective_top_k_and_sigma(stress_index)
        entries = self._mem_cache.get(agent_id, [])
        if not entries:
            return RetrievalResult(
                agent_id=agent_id,
                records=[],
                top_k_used=eff_k,
                noise_sigma_used=eff_sigma,
                friction_enabled=self.config.enabled,
                total_candidate_pool=0,
            )

        q_vec = self.embedder.embed(query_text)
        if self.config.enabled and eff_sigma > 1e-8:
            emb_noise = rng.normal(
                0.0,
                eff_sigma / math.sqrt(float(self.config.embedding_dim)),
                size=q_vec.shape,
            ).astype(np.float32)
            q_noisy = q_vec + emb_noise
            q_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(q_noisy))
            if q_norm > 1e-8:
                q_vec = q_noisy / q_norm

        # Single vectorized dot-product across all cached memories (<0.05 ms)
        e_mat = np.stack([e for _, e in entries], axis=0)
        cos_sims = e_mat @ q_vec
        ticks_arr = np.asarray([r.tick for r, _ in entries], dtype=np.float32)
        sals_arr = np.asarray([r.salience_score for r, _ in entries], dtype=np.float32)
        delta_t = np.maximum(0.0, float(current_tick) - ticks_arr)
        sal_recency = sals_arr * np.exp(-0.15 * delta_t)

        w_sal = float(self.config.recency_salience_weight)
        eff_w_sal = (
            min(0.75, w_sal + 0.35 * max(0.0, min(1.0, stress_index)))
            if self.config.enabled
            else w_sal
        )
        scores = (1.0 - eff_w_sal) * cos_sims + eff_w_sal * sal_recency
        if self.config.enabled and eff_sigma > 1e-8:
            scores = scores + rng.normal(0.0, eff_sigma, size=scores.shape)

        top_indices = np.argsort(scores)[::-1][:eff_k]
        selected = [entries[int(i)][0] for i in top_indices]

        return RetrievalResult(
            agent_id=agent_id,
            records=selected,
            top_k_used=eff_k,
            noise_sigma_used=eff_sigma,
            friction_enabled=self.config.enabled,
            total_candidate_pool=len(entries),
        )

    def _update_peer_directory(
        self,
        owner_id: str,
        peer_id: str,
        completed_signal: float,
        tick: int,
    ) -> float:
        sig = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(completed_signal)))
        conn = self._connect()
        try:
            row = conn.execute(
                """
                SELECT alpha_obs, beta_obs, interaction_count
                FROM tms_peer_directory
                WHERE owner_id = ? AND peer_id = ?;
                """,
                (owner_id, peer_id),
            ).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                new_a = 2.0 + sig
                new_b = 2.0 + (1.0 - sig)
                new_n = 1
            else:
                new_a = round(float(row["alpha_obs"]) + sig, 4)
                new_b = round(float(row["beta_obs"]) + (1.0 - sig), 4)
                new_n = int(row["interaction_count"]) + 1

            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO tms_peer_directory
                (owner_id, peer_id, alpha_obs, beta_obs, interaction_count, last_tick)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);
                """,
                (owner_id, peer_id, new_a, new_b, new_n, tick),
            )
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            self._close_if_not_shared(conn)
        return round(new_a / max(1e-6, new_a + new_b), 4)

    def _get_peer_directory_summary(
        self, owner_id: str, max_peers: int = 3
    ) -> List[Tuple[str, float, int]]:
        conn = self._connect()
        try:
            rows = conn.execute(
                """
                SELECT peer_id, alpha_obs, beta_obs, interaction_count
                FROM tms_peer_directory
                WHERE owner_id = ?
                ORDER BY interaction_count DESC, last_tick DESC
                LIMIT ?;
                """,
                (owner_id, max_peers),
            ).fetchall()
        finally:
            self._close_if_not_shared(conn)

        out: List[Tuple[str, float, int]] = []
        for r in rows:
            a = float(r["alpha_obs"])
            b = float(r["beta_obs"])
            rate = round(a / max(1e-6, a + b), 3)
            out.append((str(r["peer_id"]), rate, int(r["interaction_count"])))
        return out
